# Watermark v0: handoff for any Claude session working here

Watermark is a Krafft company: hold, show, license and protect photographs at the source. Krafft Studios' collection (Will Fullerton) is the first maker. This folder is the working app, built in a cloud session on 16 Oct 2026; the git history lives there (commits up to c9fc719). Files here are synced copies.

## Run it
- `npm install`, then `ADMIN_PASSWORD=... npm start` → http://localhost:3000 (Studio at /studio).
- `npm test` → end-to-end smoke test; must print "all flows pass".
- `node scripts/seed.js <path to TRIP_OUT_EAST_EXPORTS/EUROPE>` loads the first ten originals.

## Map
- `src/app.js` routes; `src/views.js` public pages; `src/studio-views.js` Studio; `src/rules.js` the maker's per-work terms; `src/seal.js` sealed .wmk format; `src/provenance.js` EXIF stamp + PDF certificate; `src/ledger.js` hash-chained ledger; `src/mail.js` Resend email; `public/seal-viewer.js` browser viewer for .wmk.
- Flows: one-tap license (/license/:slug → /buy → Stripe Checkout → webhook/thanks grants, emails file + certificate, maker gets "Sold"); ask-the-maker requests; renewals and expiry; sightings; maker applications; public life page /w/:slug/life; sealed files (Studio seals → /k/:id key desk → /view).
- Design record for the sealed format: `docs/SEALED.md`.
- Krafft integration (one place, served by Watermark): `public/house.js` (house bar on every site; also joins krafftco.com/license and /watermark to Watermark) and `public/k.js` (the K seal on every Krafft Studios photograph, loaded in Ghost's Code injection; matched via `/api/k` and `/api/match` from `studios_imports`). Patronage: `src/patronage.js` (/m/:slug). Series: `src/series.js`.
- The Watermark Shield: `src/credentials.js` reads Content Credentials (C2PA) on arrival and signs every delivered file (maker, copyright holder, license, no AI training; original kept as parent ingredient). Signing needs C2PA_CERT + C2PA_KEY in Render (a certificate from a CA on the C2PA trust list); without them files go out unsigned. Shield panel: `shieldBlock` in views.js (work page, life page); shield drawn on the license PDF. Test: `test/credentials.js`.
- Tips: `src/tips.js` (/tip/:post). Every Studios story carries credits (words from Ghost's author, photographs from Watermark; narration, film, music can be added in the `credits` table); a tip is shared evenly or sent to one person. Writers are maker records of kind 'writer'. The K's end band on each story shows the credits and a Tip button. Money lands in Krafft's Stripe and is recorded per person; direct payouts need Stripe Connect.
- The market: `src/market.js` (/market, /market/:id, /studio/market). Collectors offer numbered limited-edition pieces from Yours; buyers ask to buy (no charge); the Studio settles each trade by hand and records it (price, 10% royalty to the maker, 5% fee, drafts), and the buyer takes the piece through a claim link. Work price = last trade, else issue price; /yours shows the collection's value. Automatic settlement needs counsel + Stripe Connect; the license terms say non-transferable and need revising with counsel.
- Reference: a photograph at 40 MP or more (Leica Q3 60, Hasselblad X2D 100) carries the Reference badge (`refTag` in views.js; on plates and the work page) and can be bought as 'Collect the Reference' (use key `reference`, draft $25), which delivers the full original. Ordinary Collect delivers the 2400px version. Studio → Editions proposes edition sizes for approval.
- U.S. Copyright Office bridge: `src/copyright.js` (/studio/copyright). No public filing API exists, so Watermark groups works the Office's way (GRPPH: up to 750 by one author published in one year; GRUPH unpublished; $55 each), exports the title list CSV and deposit ZIP, tracks filed (service request) and registered (VA number), and writes the number onto every work in the group: 'U.S. Registered' badge (`regTag`) on plates, the work page and the Shield.
- Look closer: `src/closer.js` (/closer/:slug; `closerBlock` in views.js; `public/closer.js` reads it aloud with the browser's voice). A guide written by Claude from each held photograph and its record, filled in the background (needs ANTHROPIC_API_KEY; model CLOSER_MODEL, default claude-sonnet-5-5). Always labelled as AI; never identifies people. The maker can correct and approve (labelled approved), hide, or rewrite. Test: `test/closer.js`.
- Titles are set in title case (`src/titles.js`) on arrival and once over what is held; the maker's wording is kept in `works.title_given`. Slugs don't change.
- The contract: `src/contract.js`. Counsel's 2021 Standard Content License Agreement, assembled per order from the rights paid for (the nine permitted uses of clause 2.1; Adapt it, More than one company, On merchandise, Exclusively; the maker's never list as restrictions). Each use carries a bundle; added uses +$150, extended rights priced or asked of the maker (drafts). Buyer chooses on /license (`rightsPanel`, `public/rights.js`), reads it live (/contract/:slug), keeps it (/c/:ref/contract). Stored on `licenses.rights`. Review notes for counsel: `docs/CONTRACT.md` and /studio/contract. Old pattern came from ARCHIVE_DEV/watermark-prod license-sections.
- Market settlement: `src/market.js`. With MARKET_SETTLEMENT=stripe (after counsel, with Connect on): Buy it now / pay an accepted offer via Checkout; the piece moves at once; `payouts` (collector share, photographer royalty; the house keeps its own) paid by Connect transfers once the owner adds a bank (/yours/payouts); a second payment for a sold piece is refunded. Webhook events used: checkout.session.completed, checkout.session.expired, account.updated. Off = brokered by hand as before.
- The license page (/c/:ref, `certificate` in views.js): a receipt in plain words: what you can and can't do (from the contract), term bar, certificate of authenticity with the maker's signature, acceptance at payment, Licensing simply. Hashes only under "How we know it's genuine". Never say blockchain; the ledger is called the register in public copy (URL /ledger stays). Checkout states acceptance of the agreement; the accepted text's fingerprint is stored in licenses.rights.hash.
- Quality control: MIN_MP in app.js (share 1, story and company 12, audience and wall 24, reference 40; drafts). A use is offered only when the held file meets it (`offered`), on Watermark and on the K. Studio → Masters lists works waiting and takes a master per work (/studio/works/:slug/master: same picture by dhash, larger), via `replaceMaster`.
- Owners: `heldBy(req, w)`; the work page shows In your collection (download, license, put it up / withdraw) and leaves held uses out of the menu; /buy and one-tap refuse a second sale of a held use.
- Seats: `src/seats.js`. Each license carries seats (share 1, story 3, company 5, audience 10; drafts; extra seats $50 each via Checkout, `extra_seats` on the license): the holder adds people on the license page (/c/:ref, owner), each gets /seat/:token to keep it in their collection and take the file (delivered by `deliver`, never the holder's key); every seat recorded. Seats stay inside the licensee; another company is a new license.
- Downloads: counted per license (DL in app.js: collect 5, story 10, company 25, audience 50, print 5; drafts; `dl_extra` on the license), shared with seats, recorded in `downloads`; 410 when used up. Every license includes the share-size copy (/d/:token?size=share) and the right to share; holding any license covers Collect (`covers`).
- The ladder (`ladder` in app.js, LADDER = [1, 1.5, 2, 3]; drafts): a limited edition's Collect price steps up by quarter as it sells; the work page shows it; the market's issue price (last numbered sale) follows, so holders' values rise. Cards show value vs paid.
- The $5 piece is called Collect everywhere (use key stays `social`).
- Applause and notes: `src/applause.js`. One tip of the hat per person per work (toggle; POST /w/:slug/applaud, JSON for the page, `public/applause.js`), shown only on the work ("N tips of the hat"); Studio → Works sorts by Most applauded and Seen, never applauded (the duds, 20+ views). The guest book (table `notes`, section id #notes, styled .gb as a bound book): a line and a name (both required), no links, the plain insults turned away (UNKIND), 3 per 10 min; the maker, the Studio or the writer can remove. The signature opens the writer's collection when public. Regard: the maker marks a line 'well seen' (`seen_at`; POST /w/:slug/notes/:id/seen); a writer's lines and regard show on their collection page (In the guest books; Tips of the hat). No down-votes, no public rating of reviewers: the makers' regard is the only rating. The epigraph above the book (.nt-ep) is original house copy, a draft.
- By invitation (Will, 17 Oct): Watermark is curated like Magnum, not open like a feed. /join is the application (name, email, where the work can be seen, practice, note → `applications`); Studio → Makers approves (POST /studio/accounts with `application`) which opens the account and mails the invitation letter (`inviteLink`, `maker.invited`); the letter (/account/join/:token) opens the studio once. POST /join is closed. Formerly: /join (name + email) opened a maker account at once (signed in, `joined_at`, linked to the buyer cookie via `makers.buyer_id`); /account/profile (name, house, place, bio, site, instagram, portrait → `portraits/` in DATA_DIR, served at /portrait/:handle) shows on /m/:slug. `makers.standing` open|held: the Studio holds a maker from Accounts (their work goes private, publishing blocked) and releases them.
- Friends of the house: `src/friends.js` (/friends): Friend $10, Patron $50, Benefactor $250 a month, or any amount once (drafts), to Watermark or Krafft; wall of friends; the thanks page lets a friend set their name. Keeping (same table, kind 'keeping'): a maker's archive is free to 200 works or 5 GB (drafts), then $15/month via /account/keeping; a notice, never a block, until Will confirms. `works.bytes` records each original's size. Not tax-deductible; a foundation is the plan, said on the page.
- The maker page states the split: "85% of every sale is <first name>'s".
- Scope: photographs first. Music and film are earmarked, not built: the schema already keys works by maker and kind; add kinds when the time comes.
- The house guest book: /guestbook (`guestbook` in views.js; routes in applause.js). One book for all of Krafft in chapters (`notes.chapter`: krafft, studios, advisory, ventures, watermark, dgc; lines on photographs have work_id and count as the Watermark chapter; house lines have work_id=0). Signed from any site through the house bar's 'Guest book' link (house.js), which carries the chapter and a way back. Footer link on Watermark.
- Stars and the cull: `works.stars` 0–5 (the maker's grade; 5 sets `curated`, the jewel). Graded on the maker's studio (click or keys 1–5, 0 clears; `public/stars.js`; POST /account/works/:slug/stars), filtered by ?stars=N. POST /account/cull {from}: public works below N stars go private (never deleted; still fingerprinted and registered), published works at or above N come back. Culled work weighs a quarter toward Keeping's free line. Studio → Works sorts 'The rubies: by stars'. The standard is the Frick's: the best work in front, the rest kept.
- The guard (`public/guard.js`, on the work page via window.__WM_GUARD): a near-invisible trace lattice (accession · viewer code · watermark) over every protected image, so screenshots carry it; a quiet mark (wave + address) in the corner; when the page loses focus or Print Screen is pressed the watermark comes up at once (big wave, lattice at .35) and a nudge offers Collect. Print Screen and copy are recorded in `captures` (POST /w/:slug/capture) and shown on Studio → Works. The Mac's capture keys cannot be seen by a page; the honest line is trace + mark, not detection.
- The house bar's far right is the visitor's door: `/api/me` (CORS for *.krafftco.com and wrkfullerton.com, credentials) tells house.js who is signed in (maker, collector); the bar shows their name linking to their studio or collection, else Sign in. Will's site stays in the bar as the example of what every maker's page can be.
- Your collection shows pieces as cards (`card` in views.js; .tc in style.css).

- Sign in: `src/signin.js` (/signin). One field: email → six-digit code by mail (and a one-time link, /signin/:link), ten minutes, five tries; passkeys offered in the same field (conditional UI) and as a fallback button. The same email opens a collector's gallery and a maker's room together (`admit`), so every maker is a collector. `wm_hi` cookie shows a one-line welcome on landing. Needs RESEND_API_KEY live; without it the code is logged (tests read `app.locals.lastCode`). /signout clears all.
- Galleries: a person's collection is called their gallery everywhere in copy (/yours, /collector/:code). Each public gallery has its own guest book and hat (`gallery_hats`, notes.gallery_id; lines may be about one piece; owner removes). The house guest book excludes gallery lines.
- Trades in kind: `src/swaps.js` (/swap/new?work=slug, /swap/:id). One numbered piece for another, no money; the holder accepts; both move with fresh keys, transfers + `swap.traded` on the register, makers told. Work page: "Offer a trade for one".
- The guard (`public/guard.js`): the photograph is shown clean, nothing laid over it. Only in the capture moment (blur, visibilitychange, Print Screen, Cmd+Shift+3/4/5, copy) does the plate dim and the wave come up large with one line (accession · address) for a few seconds.
- Maker page (/m/:slug, `makerPage`): the cinematic profile after wrkfullerton.com: who, one plate, stats, Latest from Studios (`storiesBy`, Ghost by author), Series, Recent photographs, guest-book lines, the house.
- The viewing room: /wander/room (`viewingRoom`, `public/room.js`, `/api/wander` and `wanderJSON` in app.js). The nav's top-right Wander pill opens it: the photograph alone on a dark wall, the guide read aloud (G), slow auto-advance (space pauses, arrows, Esc). Made magnetic (Will, 17 Oct): the first photograph is server-rendered (`window.__WM_FIRST`, ?at=slug starts there), the next is fetched and decoded while you stand, a slow drift on each; the plate (place, title, maker) stays quietly visible; the sale is on the plate: Collect it, $5 (C), from the pocket in one tap (POST /buy with from=pocket&json=1 → {ok, no}; the room says Yours) else Checkout (?back= returns to the room); Hat (H, /w/:slug/applaud JSON) and Hang it (S, /hang/add hang=wander → the wall 'From my wandering'); standing is told to /w/:slug/stood by beacon when you move on. Full purview: the photograph fills the viewport edge to edge (no padding); the plate rises with each photograph, holds four seconds and recedes (`.vr.labeled`; a touch brings it back; never the class `plate`, which is the work page's); a deck of controls bottom centre (Back · Wander/Pause/Resume · Next · full screen); Wander asks for the browser's full screen (F toggles). /wander redirects to a random held work (curated and stars weigh more) with ?wander=1, which shows a floating "Wander on".
- The stroll (Will, 17 Oct: seeing photographs as easy and as hard to put down as Instagram, but the finest photographs): `src/stroll.js`, `public/stroll.js`. /stroll (?at=slug starts there; ?q=word strolls through one place, maker, subject): a full-screen vertical scroll-snap, one photograph per screen on the dark wall, edge to edge; the first six are in the HTML, `/api/stroll?n=&seen=&q=` brings more (never a slug the client has seen, up to 300; curated and starred weighted with chance); the next three images are prefetched; standing is told by beacon when you move on; the address follows (`?at=`). Double-tap tips your hat (the hat flies); a single tap rests the chrome (`.st-rest`); the rail: Hat (count) · Hang (From my wandering) · Collect (pocket one-tap, else POST /buy with ?back=/stroll) · Guide (a sheet, the house voice, else the browser's) · Share (Web Share, else copy). Keys j/k/arrows/space, H S G C, Esc. More of it (Will, 17 Oct): `?maker=slug` strolls one maker (a chip up top with × back to everything; a swipe left on the picture opens the maker's stroll, right returns; the maker's name on the plate too); the place on the plate strolls that place (?q=); Guide on (`[data-st-gon]`, kept in localStorage) reads each guide as you arrive; Card sends 99¢ from the pocket in one tap (POST /w/:slug/thanks json=1 → {ok} or {go}) else opens the card page; a haptic on the hat and the card where the device has one; For you: when the house knows the visitor (buyer or `wm_v`), up to three of `looking.candidates` picks are set among each page. On a phone (≤760px, coarse pointer) every Wander link leads to the stroll; the Collection's `.cx-lib` and the footer link it; sw.js never caches it. Test: smoke 'the stroll'.
- The basket (Mom, 18 Oct: "I could only collect one image; it needs a cart"): `public/basket.js` on every public page (`window.WMBasket`), routes in app.js after SETS. The basket is the cookie `wm_bk` (slugs, up to 40, 60 days; nothing reserved). 'Add to basket' (`.bk-add`) sits under Collect on the work page; a Basket act in each stroll rail; a pill with the count (`.bk-pill`, top-right in the stroll) leads to /basket: each photograph with its price and a ×, the sum, 'Collect all N' → POST /basket/collect: from the pocket in one tap when it covers (grants each, `license.paid.pocket` basket), else one Stripe Checkout with a line per photograph (set_ref `basket:<ref>`, `SETS.basket`; the webhook's `set` path and /thanks grant; /thanks clears the cookie). `basketItems` keeps only what can be collected now (held, offered, not sold out, not already the visitor's). /api/basket?s= for the client. Test: smoke 'the basket'.
- Notifications (Will, 18 Oct: "I want to know when an image of mine is licensed"): `src/notify.js`. One listener on the register (`ledger.append` is wrapped) tells the right person: license.granted paid → the maker 'X was collected/licensed · who · $ · No.'; license.requested → maker; thanks.given → maker; work.applauded → maker (off by default); maker.paid → maker; license.quoted/declined → the asking buyer; license.transferred sent → the receiver; curation.earned → the curator; guest-book lines → the maker (applause.js calls `notify.tell`). Kept in `notices` (the list at /notices, read on view; the page (Will, 18 Oct: never a stock form) stands on a photograph (the maker's best, else today's: `.ntp-hero`) with the device's state as one line and the Turn on button in it, Lately beside a sample notice when empty (`.ntx`), and the choices as switches grouped Your work / Your walls and your collecting with a line each, saved as they change (notify.js)) and pushed by Web Push to every device in `push_subs` (VAPID keys from VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY/VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY, else made once and kept in `settings`; dead endpoints dropped on 404/410). `notify_prefs` per owner (m:<id>/b:<id>) and kind. The bell (`[data-nv-bell]`, `public/notify.js`) in the header and the studio header shows the unread count (and the app badge); /notices has 'Turn on notifications' (iPhone/iPad only inside the installed app, said on the page), Send a test, Tell me when, Devices told. sw.js shows the push and opens its room on tap. The emails (Collected, Asked for) are unchanged. Test: smoke 'notifications'.
- Save to Photos (Will, 18 Oct: on a phone a download must land in Photos, not Files): `public/save.js` on every page. On a phone with Web Share for files, every file link of the house (/d/…, image a[download]) is read 'Save to Photos'; a tap fetches the file and hands it to the share sheet (Save Image → Photos); if the phone wants a fresh tap after the fetch, a sheet (`.sv`) with 'Save to Photos' takes it. The pay sheet's and tap's automatic hand-over go through the same path. A phone that opens /d/:token itself (the email link: Accept html, navigate) gets `savePage` (the photograph and one button; the file is taken, and counted, only on the tap via ?raw=1). Computers download as before.
- Curators with keys (Will, 18 Oct: the Leica gallery and Michael curate my show in Watermark): `src/curate.js`. The maker opens the whole archive (held and kept-back 'hidden') by letter from Studio → Curators (/account/curators: invite name/email/label → `curators` row + token; mailed /curate/join/:token, which signs them in as themselves via `app.locals.admit`; take back the keys any time). A gallery representing the maker (`representation` on) has the same keys (`canCurate`). The curator's desk /curate/:maker: the archive grid (filter by word and stars; kept-back marked), tap to hang into a show, the show beside it (title, statement, drag to sequence, a line per photograph), Send to the maker. A show is a hang with `for_maker_id`, `review` draft|proposed|approved|returned, `review_note`; private until approved. The maker approves on Curators or the preview (/curate/show/:id): the hang opens (public) and any kept-back work in it is published (`work.published` by:'show'); or returns it with a note. Notices: show (maker), reviewed (curator); a mail to the maker on propose. Sales through the open show pay the curator the usual curation share. Test: smoke 'curators'.
- The Watermark number (Will, 18 Oct: file names readable and beautiful, but trackable like a FedEx number): `src/track.js`. Every license ref WM-YYYY-NNNNNN reads as WM 2026 0000 8264 (year, license, two ISO 7064 mod 97-10 check digits; `track`, `ref` back from any spacing, `compact` = 202600008264). /t/:n opens it (→ /verify), /verify takes it, the license page shows it ('Anyone can check it: …', `.wm-no`). Delivered files: `captionOf` writes the EXIF ImageDescription and XMP dc:description in words ('The Staircase. A photograph by Will Fullerton, Paris, 2025. Collected on Watermark, No. 3. WM 2026 0000 8264.'), Copyright '(c) year maker. number. Look it up at …/t/…', the fingerprint moves to EXIF ImageUniqueID (32 hex) and stays in XMP; `fileName` = 'The Staircase, Will Fullerton (WM 2026 0000 8264).jpg' (ASCII filename + UTF-8 filename*). XMP carries wm:number beside wm:license. Test: smoke 'the Watermark number'.
- For the trade (Will, 18 Oct: a Getty inside Watermark, almost invisible; the professional and the visitor each to their delight): `src/trade.js`, `public/trade.js`. One switch per device (cookie `wm_trade`, /trade/on, /trade/off?back=; arriving at /trade turns it on); visitors never see any of it. /trade: a dense search (word incl. camera and accession, maker, shape landscape/portrait/square/pano, 12/24/40 MP and up, use, price ceiling, best/newest/largest; 48 a page) with the spec line, every use as a chip with its price or Ask, Comp, Lightbox (the Hang sheet: a wall is a lightbox), and a quote for a set (tick rows → use, company, email, the job → POST /trade/quote: mailed to the house, each maker and the asker; makers notified; `trade.quote` hidden). With the trade on, every photograph's page gains a spec sheet under the entry (file, accession, copyright and registration, never, credit, uses with prices, comp) and a 'Trade' chip in the header. /comp/:slug: screen size with a 'Comp, not for publication' strip and XMP, named 'Title, Maker (comp).jpg', 120 an hour per address, `comp.taken` hidden. Footer: For the trade. `app.locals.workJSON` (api.js) feeds both. Test: smoke 'for the trade'.
- See the watermark (Will, 18 Oct: the watermark is the information the picture carries; make the word ours): `src/eye.js` (GET /api/eye/:slug: number, maker, copyright, the maker's share, held (counted, never named), uses with prices or Ask, never, proof), `public/eye.js` (any `[data-eye=slug]` opens the sheet `.wye`; capture-phase so the viewer or the stroll's tap never fires under it). The eye sits top right of the work page's plate (`.eye-b`, a ghost) and of each stroll screen (`.st-eye`). The stroll's search glyph is `find`. docs/HOUSE.md 'What a watermark is' (a draft definition). Test: smoke 'see the watermark'.
- Quiet marks (Will, 18 Oct: a museum, not a brand): no K on Watermark pages (`<meta name="kh-dot" content="off">`; house.js keeps the bar hidden on phones); the wave is the mark everywhere in Watermark (`markOf` returns WAVE_MARK; K_MARK only in the house bar). The Collect mark on plates (`.pl .pb`) is the wave alone, no disc: hidden until hover on a computer, a ghost (.3) on a phone, gone while scrolling (`html.wm-scrolling`, seal.js).
- The stroll everywhere (Will, 18 Oct: the stroll is the best of the house; more of the app should feel like it): `public/tostroll.js` on every public page. On a phone (≤760px, coarse pointer) a tap on any photograph link with an image (/w/:slug or /m/<maker>/<slug>) opens /stroll?at=slug&in=<set>, taken in the capture phase ahead of the collection's lightbox; the set comes from the page (/ and /collection → collection; /m/<slug> → maker:; /g/ or /collector/ → gallery:; hang pages → hang:; /series/ → series:), else a plain stroll. Collect marks, the eye, buttons, forms and your own wall being arranged are left alone. The stroll with `in` (`inSet` in stroll.js, lists from `app.locals.walk.list`): from the tapped photograph, in the set's own order, wrapping once, never twice; the set's name is a chip up top (× and Close go back to it); at the end 'That is all of …' with Back to it and Stroll everything. Bug fixed 18 Oct: public/stroll.js had two functions named `card` (the screen builder and the 99¢ card), so loading more screens called the card and left the stroll for /thanks; the builder is now `screenFor`. Test: smoke 'the stroll through a set'.
- The letters of a sale (Will, 18 Oct: the collector's letter should arrive like an invitation to the Masters, Hermès-grade, one day the receipt for a $100,000 work; the maker's is a congratulations: joy, triumph, relief): `src/mail-letters.js` (`collected`, `sold`, `frame`), context from `letterFor(g, w, maker, u, token)` in app.js inside `grant()` (the old templates remain as the fallback if a letter throws, logged [letters]). Collector: placard line (Collected by name · No.), the title in the serif ('The Staircase is yours.'), the photograph on a cotton mount with the pencil line (No. · title, year · the maker's name), See it in your gallery (public gallery, else the license page), Save the photograph · The certificate · The agreement, What you hold (You may / You may not from `V.plainRights`, the same words as the license page), The particulars (number, held as, term, paid and date, to the maker, Watermark number), the agreement and its fingerprint, how anyone can check the papers (/t/), 'Thank you for collecting.' Maker: 'Congratulations, Will', 'The Staircase has been collected.', who chose it and how many times it and the body of work have been chosen, the mount, Yours from this sale (share, pct of fee, this month), See it in your studio, the particulars, 'Someone saw what you saw, and wanted to keep it.' Images by `/img/:slug/screen?f=jpg` (mail asks for JPEG). Preview without sending: /studio/letters (?as=maker, ?ref=). Copy is a draft for Will.
- Staying is what pays (Will, 18 Oct: the house should slow people down, like a museum; docs/HOUSE.md): in `public/stroll.js` (`arrive`, `quiet`, `hold`, `sit`): after 6 s still, `.st-still` fades the bar, plate, rail, eye and basket pill to nothing (any touch, wheel, key or pointer move brings them back); after 20 s a photograph with a guide shows its first sentence (`.st-line`); 15 arrivals inside a minute holds the next screen 3 s with 'Stay with this one.' (`.st-stay`, once a session, `wm_stay` in sessionStorage). No numbers on the wall: the rail's hat reads 'Hat', never a count. Sit (`[data-st-sit]`, the stand glyph, in the stroll's bar): each photograph held 90 s then on, the guide read, quiet music (Web Audio, four sines), wake lock, rail hidden, 'Stand up' (`.st-sitbar`) to leave.
- Full screen never crops (Will, 18 Oct: tall photographs were cut off on a computer): the stroll's image is pinned to its screen (`.st-i>.st-im` absolute, contain; a grid auto row had let it grow to full width); on a pointer:fine device the viewer fits to 92% (`center()` in viewer.js, and re-fits on resize/fullscreenchange), the viewing room and the stroll keep a margin of the dark (`.vr-im`, `.st-im` padding), and the room's drift settles from .975 to 1 instead of growing past the frame.
- The bridge (Will, 18 Oct: Watermark and Switchchord, Cole Davis's rights platform, made cohesive, so that one day they could be one; Will knows Cole well): `src/bridge.js`, design note `docs/PARTNERS.md` (a draft for Will to confirm before sharing). Open standards only, and no partner named in code or public copy: the house is did:web (`/.well-known/did.json`, the register's Ed25519 key as Multikey; `ledger.signBytes`), each maker `did:web:<host>:m:<slug>` (`/m/:slug/did.json`, with the maker's linked DID, ISNI, IPI, site in `makers.did`/`makers.ids`); licenses and authorship of record as W3C Verifiable Credentials with eddsa-jcs-2022 proofs (`/c/:ref/credential`, `/w/:slug/credential`; `prove`, `checkProof`, `jcs`, base58 in bridge.js); POST /api/v1/credentials/verify (did:web issuers resolved with a 6 s timeout). Partners (`partners`, Studio → Partners /studio/partners: name, site, DID, webhook; the key `wmp_…` shown once, stored hashed; the webhook secret shown once): GET /api/partner/v1, /api/partner/v1/makers/:slug/works (only makers who connected them: `maker_partners`; with shares and accepted claims; never releases), POST /api/partner/v1/claims (a VC + kind exclusive|administration|co-ownership|prior-license|release|other, party, uses, dates → `claims_in` pending; the maker told; accept or decline at /account/connections). An accepted exclusive holds back its uses while it runs (`app.locals.blockedBy`, consulted by `offered` and `listed`). Each paid license is posted to connected partners' webhooks, HMAC-signed (`x-watermark-signature`), with the license VC and the price, never a buyer's name (`bridge.notice` in `grant`; `partner_sends`). Maker: /account/connections (rail 'Connections'): identity, connect/disconnect each partner, claims. Shares and releases: `src/splits.js`, /account/works/:slug/splits (linked from the work record): shares of the maker's side (never more than 100%), releases (person or property, private); the statement deducts 'To your collaborators' (`splits.owedOut`, paid by hand until Connect). All of it hidden on the public register. Test: smoke 'the bridge'.
- Never a break (Will, 18 Oct): a link to a photograph no longer held (/w/:slug, /w/:slug/life, /license/:slug) redirects onward (`onward`: phones to /stroll, else /wander?from=) instead of a sign; /license with nothing to license goes to the work page. Phones (`isPhone`, by user agent, not iPad): /wander and /wander/room go to the stroll.
- The welcome (Will, 18 Oct: the curious should feel told they are allowed to look around; docs/HOUSE.md 'Made for the public'): `.cx-welcome` above the collection's intro, a draft line.
- The K on a phone (Will, 17 Oct: you need to see the images): house.js makes the bar `.kh-m` (fixed, off the top) under 760px with a coarse pointer; a small `.kh-dot` K top-left is full at the top of the page, a ghost (.55) when scrolling up or at rest, gone when scrolling down; a tap slides the bar in (`.on`); a scroll or a tap elsewhere puts it away. Applies on every Krafft site that loads house.js.
- The register page (/ledger, `src/register.js`, `registerPage`): every ledger entry told as a sentence with the photograph beside it, by day; chapters (Arrivals, Applause, Collected & licensed, Trades, Patrons, Copyright Office), spans (today … all time), oldest first. Private events are never shown. `app.locals.lately` feeds "Lately, in the house" on the home page.
- Yours in rooms: /yours?tab= gallery | files | market | books | patronage | settings (`collector`, .yt).
- /artists: every maker with held work (cover, portrait, counts); first in the nav. /copyright: the public account of the Office bridge; `copyrightState(w)` gives each work's line (registered, filed, prepared, not yet) on the work page and the Shield.
- Friends and patronage both end with the Medici ($2,500 a month or $25,000 a year, drafts) set apart, and "by letter" above it (LETTER in friends.js; the maker's email on /m/:slug).
- The door out: POST /signout → /farewell ("Fair travels. See you down the road."; words a draft). /account/logout no longer signs out on GET.
- The Shield mark (SHIELD in views.js): solid, a hairline sliver inside, the wave knocked out.
- The house voice (Will, 17 Oct: the browser's voice is not human enough): `src/speech.js`. Every guide is read once by a neural voice and kept as an MP3 in the vault (`speech/<slug>-<hash>.mp3`, table `speech`), served at /closer/:slug/audio (404 when none and no provider: the page falls back to the browser's voice). Provider by env: OPENAI_API_KEY (model SPEECH_MODEL default gpt-4o-mini-tts; SPEECH_VOICE default ash; STYLE = a museum guide, unhurried) or ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (SPEECH_VOICE = voice id). `fill()` 45 s after start then half-hourly reads every guide without a current recording, best work first; a corrected guide is read again (hash of provider+voice+text). `public/closer.js`, `public/room.js` (`wanderJSON.audio`) and the viewer (through the page's button) play the recording first, lighting paragraphs by length. /healthz `speech`. Test: `test/speech.js`.
- The study (Will, 18 Oct: the best art history resource there is; the friend of the most curious; teach people how to think about art): beneath each guide's wall text, `study` in `works.closer` JSON, written by `study(w)` in closer.js in two steps: research (RESEARCH_SYSTEM, Anthropic web search tool `web_search_20250305`, override CLOSER_SEARCH_TOOL, max 5 searches, notes with sources; a 400 skips research) then the write through a tool, asked for, not forced, since the live model refuses a forced tool_choice; text JSON read when no tool call (STUDY_SYSTEM, `STUDY_TOOL` write_study with a fixed schema, so it always arrives whole; `parseStudy` keeps only https links; `read` = the pages the search opened). Sections (`studyBlock` in views.js, `.sd`, details drawers): What to look for (3, open), the place and its story, How it was made, In conversation with (artists), Words to keep, To carry with you (questions), Read further; Stroll this place; 'Researched on the web and written with AI'. `fill()` writes studies after guides, best work first, STUDY_PER_RUN (40) each half hour; failures mark `studyMissed`. The maker: Research and write the study / again, Hide/Show the study, a preview on /closer/:slug. The stroll's guide sheet links 'Go further' to /m/<maker>/<slug>#study (`wanderJSON.study`). Test: test/closer.js.
- The guide as much or as little as you want (Will, 18 Oct: never forced; the treasure easy to find; the chess engine that sharpens your own move, then you turn it off): one setting for the whole house, `html[data-guide]` = off | hints (default) | full, set before paint from localStorage `wm_guide` (inline script in `layout`), switched by `GUIDE_SWITCH` (views.js; in the Look closer header and the stroll's guide sheet; handled in public/closer.js `WMGuide` and in stroll.js). Off folds the guide to one slim line ('Look closer' + its first sentence + Open, `.lc-min`) and the stroll shows no line; Hints shows the wall text's first paragraph with Read on, and the study's What to look for as a coach ('Find each one in the picture before you read why', each why behind 'Why it matters', `[data-sd-reveal]`), other drawers closed; Full opens every drawer and the stroll's line comes at 7 s.
- Look closer guides carry `sources` (one a line) and `by` (credit), set on /closer/:slug; shown under the text. The system prompt writes museum wall text for first-time lookers.
- Two front doors (Will, 18 Oct: Studios is the magazine, Watermark is the thing; one day krafft.com and watermark.com; advisory a subpage; the rest tucked away): the house bar (`public/house.js`, and `HOUSE` in views.js) shows only Studios and Watermark (`front: 1` in P) and your door; the K opens `.kh-menu`, 'The house of Krafft': Krafft (the company: advisory, ventures), Dallas Golf Club, Will Fullerton, Guest book. On Studios the bar is not a strip above the magazine: it is set inside the theme's own top row (`.k-nav__inner`, `.kh-embed`), so the magazine has one bar. Domain moves (krafft.com, watermark.com) are Will's; the hosts live in P and BASE_URL.
- Paying in place: `public/pay.js` (Express Checkout: Apple Pay, Google Pay, Link; one tap; 'Pay by card instead' and no-wallet devices go to Stripe Checkout). The wallets only show when the domain is registered with Stripe: the app serves `/.well-known/apple-developer-merchantid-domain-association` (fetched from stripe.com, cached) and `registerDomain()` (30 s after start, live https only) creates or re-validates the payment method domain for BASE_URL's host; logged as [stripe] domain.
- /healthz reports which services are on by name (mail, closer, stripe as live | test | false, signing). render.yaml declares RESEND_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY, MAIL_FROM.
- Wallets: every gallery has an address (`buyers.code`, shown as WM-code on Yours → Show it, or keep it). POST /yours/send {ref, to: address|email} moves a numbered piece wallet to wallet at once (fresh key, transfer, `license.transferred` how:'sent'), or mails a claim link to an unknown email. `canTransfer(l)` (app.locals) reads `licenses.rights.transferable`, else: Collect/Reference/print yes, working licenses only when the work's rules say `transfer`. Enforced on send, give, swaps, market. Contract 9.3 carries the carve-out (counsel; docs/CONTRACT.md DRAFTED).
- The register is signed: `ledger.js` derives an Ed25519 key from REGISTER_SEED (else VAULT_KEY, else the session secret) and signs every entry's hash (`ledger.sig`); /ledger.json publishes the house public key; `checkSig`. `verify` checks only entries after the last verified head (full walk hourly; `verifyAll` for tests).
- Standing: `src/firsts.js` (`stood` table). The guard posts /w/:slug/stood with the viewer code (also mirrored to the `wm_v` cookie); the first hundred are told on the page; Yours → Gallery shows "Your looking, this month" privately; sign-in claims earlier looking.
- Today's photograph: `todays()` in app.js (deterministic by date from curated/4-star works), on the front door and at /today.
- The Hall of Patrons: `src/hall.js` (/patrons; Studio → Back office → Hall of Patrons). A portrait is a held work hung by slug with name, founded, year, note; brass plate beneath. No token, no cryptocurrency: rights are tracked and traded in dollars.
- Keeping in tiers (`TIERS` in friends.js; drafts): The studio free to 200 photographs or 5 GB; Keeping $15/mo to 2,000 or 100 GB; The archive $45/mo to 10,000 or 500 GB; The vault $150/mo to 50,000 or 2 TB; above by letter. /account/keeping shows the meter (used vs the tier's line; culled work a quarter) and the ladder; begin via Checkout subscription, move in place (`stripe.subscriptions.update`, `keeping.moved`); a notice, never a block. `friends.level` holds the tier key.
- Rembrandt's room: a maker's five-star held works hang in their own gallery (/yours, /collector/:code) as "From <name>'s own hand", the artist's copies, above "What <name> collects" (`ownHand` in app.js; maker found by `makers.buyer_id` or email, else the signed-in maker). A signed-in maker without a gallery is given one on /yours.
- Claims (copyright or consent disputes): `src/disputes.js`. The door on every work page (/w/:slug/claim: kind, name, email, evidence, optional file, good-faith statement under penalty of perjury; 3 a day per address). On arrival `works.disputed` > 0: shown, not sold (`offered`/`listed` refuse; the page says Under review); claimant, maker and the house mailed. The maker answers at /account/claims/:id (dashboard Needs you). Studio → Back office → Claims (/studio/claims/:id) shows the claim, the answer and the packet (maker of record, arrival and register entry, date made, camera, fingerprint, size, Content Credentials, Copyright Office, fixity, holders) and decides: dismiss (sales resume), uphold (work goes private, `dispute.upheld` told on the register without names, holders counted), withdrawn. `dispute.opened/answered/dismissed/withdrawn` hidden. Terms carry a Claims clause; agent address house@watermark.krafftco.com (HOUSE_EMAIL). Counsel to review before relied on; DMCA agent registration with the Copyright Office is Will's.
- Curators and hangs: `src/hangs.js`. A collector holds the work; a curator hangs it: a titled, ordered wall of photographs from the whole collection, a line on each (`hangs`, `hang_works`), private until shown; /hangs, /hang/:slug (walked one photograph at a time on a dark wall), Yours → Hangs (make, edit, order, show; the curator's statement `buyers.statement`), 'Hang it' on every work page (POST /hang/add). Visiting a shown hang or a public gallery sets `wm_via` (30 days); /buy and /license stamp `licenses.via`; on a paid grant `reward()` credits CURATOR_PCT (5%, confirmed by Will 17 Oct, from the house's side, never the maker's; a public gallery's link earns the same) to the curator's pocket (`curation.earned`, hidden), `hangs.sales/earned`. A card for the curator (99¢+, pocket or Checkout, `hang_thanks`, metadata.hangthanks → `thanks_in` in the curator's pocket). /collectors is 'Galleries': Hangs then Collections. `hang.opened` told on the register. Studio → Back office → Hangs (/studio/hangs): every wall, feature (leads the front door and the Galleries), show/hide; the house hangs walls of its own by slug (curated under the house maker's gallery, `houseBuyer`; earns nothing). The Studio may edit the house's walls.
- The cabinet (Will, 17 Oct: collectors photograph the art they own and share it with discretion): `src/cabinet.js`. `cabinet` (a collector's own works: title, artist, year, medium, dims, private note, file in the vault `cabinet/`, fingerprint; 200 a cabinet, 60 MB a file, drafts), `showings` (slug, title, line, access private|guests|public, opens_at/closes_at: a run), `showing_works`, `showing_guests` (email, name, token, seen_at, visits). Yours → Cabinet (put in, edit, new showing); /show/:slug walked like a hang; the owner's desk on it (access, run, guests by letter: POST /show/:id/guests mails each a link /show/:slug/g/:token that admits them and is remembered in `wm_g`; remove a guest and the link dies; a signed-in collector whose email is on the list is admitted too). Images at /cab/:id/:size only to the owner or someone admitted to a showing it hangs in; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, noindex; sw.js never caches /show, /cab. The house never says where a work hangs; nothing of the cabinet is told on the register (`cabinet.kept`, `showing.opened` hidden). Not yet: offering a cabinet work for prints or licence (the collector owns the object, not the copyright, unless public domain or they hold the rights: counsel first).
- Introductions (Will, 17 Oct: the referral network comes from artists, curators, museums): `src/introductions.js`. `introductions` (token, by_kind house|maker|buyer, by_id, label, opens maker|collector, uses, max_uses, closed). /i/:token: a page in the introducer's name; name + email opens a maker's studio at once (signed in, `makers.introduced_by` = maker:<handle> | house | buyer:<code>, `maker.introduced` told on the register "introduced by …", welcome mailed, the introducer told) or a collector's gallery (`buyers.introduced_by`). A maker gives MAKER_INTROS_A_YEAR (5, draft) maker introductions a year, good once each, collector introductions without limit (Your page → Introductions); the Studio issues partner links with a label and a cap (Studio → Accounts → Introductions, `introduction.issued` hidden); every collector may introduce collectors (POST /yours/introductions). The maker page says "Introduced to the house by …".
- Addresses belong to people (Will, 17 Oct): a photograph is canonical at /m/<maker-slug>/<slug> (route rewrites to /w/:slug; a wrong maker 301s; /w/:slug still answers); a gallery at /g/<code> (alias of /collector/:code); a hang at /g/<code>/hang/<slug>, a showing at /g/<code>/show/<slug> (`hangHref`, `href` in cabinet.js; slugs unique, no random suffix for new ones; bare /hang/:slug and /show/:slug still answer). Sub-pages (/w/:slug/life, /claim, /thanks…) stay on /w/.
- The pocket cashes out: `credit()`, `earned()`, `cashable()` (earned kinds: curation, thanks_in, earnings, resale; never top-ups). POST /pocket/cashout: a Connect transfer when PAYOUTS=stripe and `buyers.stripe_account` is set (POST /pocket/connect, Express), else recorded and the house mailed to pay by hand. `pocket.cashed` hidden. Will (17 Oct): everybody can buy work or cash out.
- The bar: Artists · Collection · Galleries · Market · Yours. The front door carries a "Walls hung by people with an eye" band (up to three public hangs) above Lately. Series and Journal sit inside the Collection (`.cx-lib`) and the footer.
- The house in a maker's studio: `?maker=handle` sets `wm_as` (12 h, Studio only); `makerOf` reads it when no maker cookie, so the house walks every /account room; cleared on /signout.
- The catalogue card: the work record (`workTerms`) opens with the plate in its case, the numbers (earned, licensed, waiting, sightings) and links; the record below folds into tabs (`public/desk-tabs.js`: every top-level h2 inside `[data-desk-tabs]`; hash or ?ok picks the tab; without the script it reads top to bottom).
- The desk: `deskHeader` in views.js (Studio shell; maker rail for /account*); `setDeskPath`, `setDeskStatus`. Studio → Works has The register (table) beside The wall. The vault (/account/upload) is the ceremony of putting work in.
- The jewel box: plates sit in a pool of light on a near-black wall with a hairline edge; lights come up on load. The viewing room stands first (W to wander). /patrons, /farewell, the 404 (`notFound`).
- The archival master: `src/archive.js`, design record `docs/ARCHIVE.md`. `.wmark` = the original byte for byte + signed manifest + register extract + certificate of deposit, stored zip (`/w/:slug/archive.wmark`; maker, Studio, Reference holders). `/verify` takes a `.wmark` or any image and reports (signature, fingerprint, held today, register). Fixity: originals re-hashed on a schedule (`fixity` table; `app.locals.archive.fixity()`); Shield row 'Archival master'; Studio → Conservation (/studio/fixity); failures mailed to the house, `fixity.failed` on the register (hidden).
- The desk you can clear (Will, 17 Oct: processing requests should feel like something you can do all day): /account/requests opens with Waiting and Earned this month and a 'Clear the desk' door to the oldest request; each request page shows its place in the queue (n of N, Next), the fee pre-filled from what the maker last took for that use, else their price, with 'your share is yours' live; keys A accept, G grant free, D decline (focus the word), N next, Esc the desk; after any answer `onward()` redirects to the next one waiting (the note shown there), or to the desk (All clear). With mail off an accepted request stays on its page to show the payment link. Getting paid is a statement (left) beside two doors (right: Into your pocket, Your bank; `.gp-cols`); Keeping's tier headings read in the serif.
- Requests: `src/requests.js`. A use the maker approves, has no price for, or that the held file is too small for (MIN_MP) is 'requested'; the maker answers at /account/requests (accept at a fee → payment link; grant free; decline with a word; add the master on the spot); Studio → Licenses answers through the same helpers; the buyer is mailed either way. The work page lists every allowed use (`listed`), small-file ones as Ask.
- The Library: `src/library.js` (/library, /library/:slug, read PDFs inline at /library/:slug/read; take a copy: POST /library/:slug/take → Checkout (metadata.library) → /library/d/:token, PDF held in the vault `texts/` or set from the text; Studio → Library). `texts`, `text_orders`; `text.shelved`, `text.taken` on the register. Regard: `src/regard.js` (no down-votes; the collection, artists and the maker's body of work hang by it; `chronology`). The house standard: `docs/HOUSE.md`.
- The app: installable PWA. iPhone, iPad, Mac (Will, 17 Oct): `viewport-fit=cover`; in standalone/fullscreen the header, decks, plates, sheets and footer pad by `env(safe-area-inset-*)`; a Back button (`.nv-back`, `[data-nv-back]`) shows in the header only when installed, on inner pages reached from the house; /app puts this device's own instructions first (`[data-os]`, `.ap-me`). App Store: not a listing of the PWA; a native shell (Capacitor) is possible later, but Apple takes 15–30% of digital sales made in-app and requires its own purchase flow, so the shell would show and the web would sell. `public/app/manifest.webmanifest` + icons (the wave on black, made with sharp), `public/sw.js` (served at /sw.js: shell cache-first, /img cached up to 400, pages network-first with the last copy kept, /offline room; nothing private cached), Speculation Rules prerender on hover for public pages, View Transitions cross-fade, /app (install page; `beforeinstallprompt` button), footer link. Bump `V` in sw.js when the shell changes.
- The price is not on the wall: `.seal-t b em` fades in on hover/focus (and on a first tap on touch, `px-on`). The thank-you card (`/w/:slug/thanks` in tips.js: 99¢, $4.99, $9.99 or any ≥ 99¢, a word; `tips` rows with role 'thanks', post_slug 'work:<slug>'; `thanks.given` on the register; the house keeps none). The pocket: `src/pocket.js` (`pocket` table; top-ups $10/$25/$50 via Checkout, metadata.pocket; `spend()`; Yours → Settings `pocketBlock`); a card the pocket covers is posted at once with no checkout. Payout of cards to makers is with their earnings (by hand; Connect later). Crypto/fractional payments: Will says someday, not now.
- Getting paid: `src/payouts.js` (/account/payouts). The maker's statement: share_pct of paid licenses + thank-you cards and tips whole + royalties − `maker_payouts`. Bank via Stripe Connect Express (`makers.stripe_account`, /account/payouts/connect). Transfers only when `PAYOUTS=stripe` is set (after Connect is on): on the 1st (6-hourly check), on account.updated, or POST /account/payouts/pay; otherwise paid by hand and recorded at POST /studio/payouts/:handle. `maker.paid` on the register. Dashboard 'Needs you' shows what is owed.
- Alexandria: `src/open.js`. `works.open` = pd | cc0 | cc-by | cc-by-sa | cc-by-nc (set on the work's terms page, maker or Studio; `work.opened`/`work.closed`). An open work offers no sale; its page shows the licence, 'Take the original' (/open/:slug/take, no key, `open.taken` on the register) and the credit line. The shelf: /open (linked beside Library inside the Collection). Makers of kind 'institution' (museums, libraries, archives) are invited from Studio → Accounts and listed with the artists.
- The switch: `SWITCH` in views.js. Makers: Studio | Gallery on the studio header and on /yours. The house (Studio cookie): The house | My studio | Gallery, on the back office header and on /yours (`setDeskAdmin` per request).
- The desk, set like a registrar's book: fixed 56×42 thumbnails in a small case on every row (.hd-row, .ac-row, .cat-th, .tbl, .rq, .lg-row); Studio → Licenses is a ledger grouped Waiting / Awaiting payment / Ending within thirty days / Live / Ended (`licenses` in studio-views, `.lg-row`). Yours → Files & licenses opens with 'Your rights' (`rights` from /yours: every working license with Covered until, people, downloads left, License · Agreement · File · Renew; `.cov-row`). The house's 'My studio' opens the house's own maker (`setHouseMaker`). The Hall desk row is `.hl-row` (not .hd-row).
- The mark, policy with no switch (Will, 17 Oct): `src/mark.js`; `marked()` in app.js wraps every image that leaves the house (licensed deliveries and seats via `deliver`, free-and-clear takes in open.js). Three layers: in the open, XMP (dc:creator/rights, xmpRights:Marked/WebStatement=/c/:ref, UsageTerms, photoshop:Credit, plus:Licensor (/license/:slug)/CopyrightOwner/ImageSupplierImageID (accession), Iptc4xmpExt:RegistryId, DigitalSourceType, wm:license/accession/register) written as an APP1 segment with the EXIF stamp (provenance.js); sealed, Content Credentials when C2PA_CERT/KEY are set; within the picture, an invisible mark (8×8 block brightness shifts ±3 placed by MARK_KEY (env; else the session secret), 40 bits = the `downloads.id` + check, repeated hundreds of times; survives recompression and screenshots at the same size, not heavy resize or crop; JPEG re-encoded at q92 4:4:4, PNG/TIFF lossless). Share-size copies also carry a visible credit strip (`credit()`: © maker · watermark.krafftco.com/c/ref). Every delivery records `downloads.sha256`, `mark`, `work_id`, `to_whom`; `app.locals.mark.trace(buf)` reads bytes → XMP → mark and /verify says "This file left the house on …, under license …, delivered to …" (who has it). The archival master (.wmark) stays byte for byte, by design. Tests: smoke (delivery, recompressed trace, /verify).
- The open API (Will, 17 Oct: the company everybody can plug into): `src/api.js`, /developers. Read-only, CORS open, 600 requests a minute per address (guardrails limiter), nothing personal (a licensee's company may be named, a person never; /verify answers drop the holder's name). GET /api/v1 (index), /api/v1/works/:slug (`workJSON`: maker, year, place, credit line, copyright and registration, fingerprint, register entry, Content Credentials, never list, uses with prices and instant/ask, images, pages, embed), /api/v1/works?q=&maker=&limit=&offset=, /api/v1/makers/:slug (with standing), /api/v1/licenses/:ref (the check a platform runs before showing a copy: status, use, term, work, company), /api/v1/verify (POST file via `verifyBuffer`, or ?sha256= against originals and `downloads.sha256`), /api/v1/match?src= (Krafft image address or sha256), /api/v1/credit/:slug (text, HTML, short), /api/v1/register/:slug (signed public entries; HIDDEN exported from register.js), /oembed?url= (rich with `<watermark-image>`, or photo) with discovery `<link rel="alternate" type="application/json+oembed">` on work pages. Partner keys: see The bridge (src/bridge.js).
- The atelier (Will, 17 Oct: studio tools, libraries, resources; Leica-grade education): `src/atelier.js`, /account/atelier (makers and the Studio), `guides` table seeded with six house guides in four sections (The craft: scanning film, developing black and white; The archive: cataloguing, professionalising the archive; The business: registering copyright, pricing and licensing; Looking: how to look at a photograph, with a short history and reading list), all drafts until Will confirms (Studio edits and confirms on the guide page; POST /studio/atelier/:slug; new guides via do=new). Text is paragraphs with **bold**/*italic*/`code` only. Rail: Your studio · Requests · The vault · Your page · Getting paid · Atelier · Your gallery (Keeping linked from Getting paid and the dashboard).
- Standing by the edit (Will, 17 Oct: the great collectors culled ruthlessly): `standing(db, makerId, {from})` in regard.js = regard of what is shown ÷ √(how much is shown), so a tight edit outranks a long wall. /artists ranks by it; the maker dashboard's "The edit" card shows standing now, what it would be culling from 3/4/5 stars (with the best marked, real numbers), and "You show N of M held"; the maker page says "Shows N of M held: the edit is the work."
- The archival print (Will, 17 Oct): the license page's figure (`certificate`, `.lc2-print`/`.pr-*`) shows the photograph on a cotton mount with a pencil line (edition number, title and year, the maker's signature) and the wave blind-stamped; the holder's "Take the file" sits beneath with what the file carries. The K on Studios (`/api/k`, `public/k.js`) now lists every use as on the work page (instant by price, the rest marked Ask), links to the canonical /m/<maker>/<slug>, and the end band offers "Wander from here" (the viewing room at the story's first held photograph).
- The signs (Will, 17 Oct: never a number, always a photograph and the way on): `sign()` and `SIGNS` in views.js (400, 401 with Sign in, 403, 404, 409, 410, 413, 429, 500, 502, 503: a card like a museum's gallery door). In app.js an early middleware rewrites `res.sendStatus(4xx/5xx)` for HTML GETs (not /api, /img, /cab, /static, /d, /portrait) into a sign with a random landscape cover (`coverFor`); the final 404 and the error handler (`app.use((err…))`, logged as [house]) do the same; JSON and images keep plain statuses. The Ask page keeps the menu of every other use (`usesFor`, `.buy-menu`) and the way back. sw.js: a 502–504 on a navigation shows the last cached copy or the /offline room ("Between rooms"), so a deploy never shows Render's page to a returning visitor.
- Phones (Will, 17 Oct): no page may be wider than the screen (the white bar was `.cx-bar`, the search-and-views row). Under 760px the bar is a grid: search on its own line, then the rooms, makers and the view links each in a sideways-scrolling row; the header wraps to two lines (mark + Wander, then the rooms); the house bar shows K · Studios · Watermark · Sign in; the viewing room keeps its plate above the deck. Check with a 390px viewport: `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` must equal `innerWidth` on every public page.
- The frame (Will, 17 Oct: photographs cycling on a television, a Samsung Frame for the house, chosen for the evening, with music): `src/frame.js`, `public/frame.js`. /frame is the door; /frame/tv (alias /tv) is the screen: a five-letter code (no vowels) until a remote takes it, then the set plays full screen (two images cross-fading, the next decoded while one hangs, a slow drift, the plate for a moment; space pauses, arrows move, F fills, Esc leaves; wake lock); ?set=… plays at once. /frame/pair?code= → /frame/:code, the remote (the first signed-in hand holds it, `frames.buyer_id`): what to show (today: curated/4★; my gallery; a series; a wall hung by a curator (public or mine); one maker; or ask for a word, `q:`), change every 15 s–5 min, plate always/brief/never, music none | quiet (made in the browser with Web Audio: four sines breathing, nothing streamed, nothing to license) | own (a stream the viewer may play), a title. The screen polls /frame/tv/:code/state every 5 s. Held pieces show at `large` without the plate's credit; the rest at `screen` with "· Watermark" on the plate. Collect the set: every unheld piece's Collect ($5), one tap from the pocket (`spend`, grant each, `license.paid.pocket` with frame) or one Checkout with set_ref `frame:<code>:…` (the webhook's `set` path grants). Licence copy (draft): at home a screen is a wall, free at screen size; public display by letter. sw.js never caches /frame/*, /tv. Footer and the Collection's `.cx-lib` link it. Test: smoke 'the frame'.
- Galleries and the eye (Will, 17 Oct: curation is a badge of honour first; the first curators are real galleries selling legitimate shows): `src/galleries.js`. A gallery is a maker of kind 'gallery' (Studio → Accounts; rail gains 'The gallery' via `setDeskGallery`). `representation` (gallery_id, maker_id, pct of the maker's side, DEFAULT_PCT 50 draft, offered|on|declined|ended): the gallery offers by handle from /account/gallery (or its standing introduction link, `introductions.linkFor`, represents whoever arrives through it at once); the maker answers at /account/representation (dashboard Needs you; one gallery at a time). On every paid license of a represented maker's work `cut()` records `gallery_cuts` (price × maker share × pct; the house's 15% untouched; `representation.earned` hidden): the gallery's statement gains 'Representation', the maker's 'To your gallery' (payouts.js). Shows: `hangs.opens_at/closes_at/venue` set on the wall (POST /hang/:id/show; `show.opening` told); a gallery's hangs read 'A show · Gallery · now showing/opening soon/closed', 'Mounted by'; `onNow()` leads /collectors (Galleries) with 'Shows'; the gallery's /m/ page lists Represents and Shows, a represented maker's says 'Represented by'. The eye (`eye(b)`): one line of standing for any curator, walls hung · placed (paid sales via their hangs or gallery link, not their own) · well seen; badges (drafts) A gallerist · A dealer (10 placed, 3 walls) · An eye (1 and 1) · A curator; shown on hang pages (`.hg-eye`) and Galleries cards (`.fc-eye`). No leaderboard. `hangs.ensure` and /yours give a signed-in maker their own gallery (by buyer_id or email) before any anonymous one. Test: smoke 'galleries'. Counsel: the representation split and the gallery's standing in the contract.
- The glyphs (Will, 17 Oct: the hat, the hang and the rest were underwhelming): `src/icons.js`, one family on a 24-point grid, 1.5 line, round ends (hat: a top hat tipped; hang: a frame on a nail by its cord; collect: the wave in a ring; page, guide, stand, trade, send, card, back/next, pause/play, full, close, wave, key, eye, in/out, grip, plus, check). Used with a word beside them. Wander in the bar is the wave and the word, no capsule (`.top .nv-w`). The viewing room's deck is one frosted bar bottom-right (back · ● Wander · next | full), no capsules; Guide/Standing/close top-right as glyph + word; the hint is bare text. Esc in the room returns to `data-from`.
- Seamless to full screen (Will, 17 Oct): `public/viewer.js` lifts the photograph from where it hangs on the work page (FLIP from the plate's rect, the page's own image first, the large file swapped in once there) to full screen in one motion and sets it back the same way on close; the dark comes up around it (`.wv.lit`). The viewer is a room (Will, 17 Oct: it had got thin): nothing over the picture; a pool of light (`.wv-vig`); the plate (title, credit · place, year, place in the walk) rises after the lift, holds four seconds and recedes (`.wv.labeled`; a tap or a movement brings it back); the deck bottom centre: Before · Hat · Hang it · Collect · Next (the page's own `.ap-b`, `.hg-open`, `form.share-buy` do the work, so pocket one-tap holds); top: Guide (the Look closer text shown in a panel and read aloud through the page's `[data-say]`), − +, Close; keys ← → H S C G Esc. Glyphs reach the script as `<template data-ic=…>` on the work page.
- Hanging like saving (Will, 17 Oct: as intuitive as Pinterest): `public/hang.js`. 'Hang it' (`.hg-open`, in `hangIt`) opens a sheet (`.hs`): a line for a new wall at the top, then your walls (GET /api/hangs/mine: id, title, n, public, href, thumb); tap a wall → POST /hang/add (json) → 'Hung' and 'See the wall'; no page change. On your own wall the plates carry a grip and are arranged by dragging (mouse or touch); POST /hang/:id/order {order: slugs} saves (`.hg-own`, `data-arrange`). Earlier/Later buttons are gone.
- The walk (Will, 17 Oct: from one photograph to the next inside what you came from): `src/walk.js`, `public/walk.js`. Listing pages set `wm_in` (2 h): collection, maker:<handle> (/m/), gallery:<code> (/collector, /g), hang:<slug>, series:<slug>; `?in=` overrides. `walk(req, slug)` places the photograph in that list (regard order for the collection and archives; collector number for galleries; pos for walls; chapters for series; lists cached 60 s): the work page carries `walkBar` under the plate (before · "Will Fullerton's archive, 3 of 12" · next, with thumbnails; wraps) and `window.__WM_WALK`; ← → keys and a swipe on the plate or in full screen move; the next page and image are prefetched. In full screen an arrow opens the next photograph already lifted (`?open=1` → viewer click on load), so a walk can be taken entirely full screen.
- Hand it to your phone (Will, 17 Oct: seamless between devices): /signin/hand (signed-in maker or collector with an email) shows a QR (`src/qr.js`: byte mode, level M, versions 1–10, built from ISO 18004, verified round-trip) of a one-time ten-minute sign-in link (`signins` row with no usable code, tries exhausted); the phone that scans it is admitted as them (`admit`: the same gallery, studio, pocket). 'Mail me the link' when mail is on. Linked from Yours → Settings ('Your devices', `.dev`) and the studio header ('Your phone', `.ac-hand`). Passkeys remain the other way.
- Your looking (Will, 17 Oct: a year of looking, told back, like a listening service): `src/looking.js`. `stood.visits` counts returns (a beacon with s=0 on an existing row). /yours/looking (this month | all time): photographs stood before, time in all, among the first hundred, hats, collected, walls; 'The one that held you' (longest, with visits); Longest in front of; You keep coming back to; You lean toward (makers); Places; Your hour. The letter: on the first of the month (daily timer, `letters()`, once per buyer per month, `letters` table) mails everyone with an email who stood before something last month. For you (`forYou`, `/api/for-you?n=`): a curator's eye, never the same twice: candidates exclude what they stood before and what they were shown in 14 days (`shown` table; a small house falls back to shown-but-not-stood), scored by likeness (place, maker, keywords, weighted by seconds) with half the shortlist from further off; when ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is set Claude chooses from the shortlist and writes one line why ("Because you stood before the dome for four minutes"), labelled 'with AI'; else likeness with varied lines. Shown as 'For you' on the front door and Yours → Gallery (`public/foryou.js` fills `[data-for-you]` after load) and under the record. Nothing of a person's looking is told to anyone.
- Pictures by the byte (17 Oct): `/img/:slug/:size` answers WebP when the browser accepts it (`images.pick`, `Vary: Accept`; q 70/76/78 thumb/screen/large, about 40% under the JPEG), else JPEG; renditions written atomically. `warmAll` (app.locals; 15 s after start, then hourly) makes every size in both formats for every held work ahead of the first visitor (`images.warm`/`warmed`); `replaceMaster` clears both. Next for speed: a CDN in front of /img and /static (Cloudflare; Will's DNS decision), srcset on plates, SQLite on the persistent disk with WAL.
- Efficiency: compression on; /api/index.json serves the collection's search index with an ETag once the library passes 300 works (collection.js fetches it); below that it ships inline.

- The beta and the test house (Will, 18 Oct: it is live but still a beta; build it for testing, then launch it properly): `src/beta.js`. Until Studio → Launch says launched (`settings.launched`, POST /studio/launch/state; `V.setLaunched`), a quiet 'Beta' sits by the wordmark and the footer says 'Tell us' → /feedback (kind, what happened, optional email, hidden honeypot, 6 per 10 min; mailed to the house) read at Studio → Feedback (/studio/feedback, mark done). Studio → Launch gains 'Before the announcement' (maker's notes, catalogue notes and guest lines on the ten in front, full masters, outside makers, feedback answered; the test house, uptime and counsel by hand). `STAGING=1` is the test house: a band on every page, noindex and robots Disallow, a live Stripe key refused, mail only to MAIL_ALLOW with '[Test house]' subjects; Will's steps in docs/STAGING.md (not in render.yaml, so a blueprint sync never creates a paid service). Launch fixes (18 Oct, watermark-launch-fixes.md in 00-INBOX): Shield 'Held by Watermark for', master row only at 12 MP+, counts on the wall only from five, Lately grouped, rooms as places (the series before a colon dropped), nav Collection · Makers · Sign in/Yours, `works.note` (the maker's note above the guide, 'Read with AI'), memory caps (sharp cache/concurrency, MALLOC_ARENA_MAX, heap 320 MB). Test: `test/beta.js`.

- Bring a photograph home (Will, 18 Oct: drop in a Watermark image and, when you hold the rights, it joins your gallery; collectors add what they were given by people able to give it): `src/drop.js`. /yours/add (a drop zone; linked from Yours → Gallery and from /verify) reads the file the way /verify does (bytes, XMP, the mark within; or a .wmark) and answers: already yours; licensed to your email on another door → gathered into this gallery (`license.transferred` how:'gathered'); held by someone else and passable (`canTransfer`, numbered) → 'Ask them to pass it on' (`pass_requests`, the holder told by notice kind 'asked' and mail, /pass/:token: Pass it on → fresh key, `license.transferred` how:'given', the receiver told; Keep it); not passable → collect your own; no license → collect it; unknown. Nothing moves without the holder's yes. `license.pass.asked/kept` hidden on the register. Test: test/drop.js and smoke.

## Rules (from Will)
- Restrained, precise voice; no hype. Anything written in Will's voice is a draft until he confirms.
- Draft prices: In a story $350, For a company $750, Before an audience $2,500, On a wall $250. "85% to the maker" on the home page is a draft.
- Never paste secrets (Stripe keys, passwords) into chat or code; they go in the host's environment settings.
- Sealed format: built only from public standards. Do NOT open, read or reuse anything from C-Hear / CHIF materials (00-INBOX/…/17-C-Hear). Will signed an NDA with C-Hear in May 2022 that bars use and reverse engineering.
- People in the work (Will, 29 Oct): street work and portraits may hang, be collected, printed and go in a book of the maker's work without a release; a company's or a campaign's use waits for a release (clearance.js enforces it). The maker keeps back any picture or person they choose (`works.keep_back`); a person shown may ask for a picture to come down (the claims door). Children get a look before hanging, never a block. No face recognition, ever. Child safety: docs/CHILD-SAFETY.md.
- Deleting files in Will's folders isn't permitted from the cloud session; leftovers go to `_to_delete`.

## Next
1. Deploy: private GitHub repo → Render Blueprint (render.yaml) → BASE_URL https://watermark.krafftco.com, ADMIN_PASSWORD, SESSION_SECRET, STRIPE_SECRET_KEY, STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET → DNS CNAME.
2. Stripe test mode end to end; then Resend for email.
3. Load the full Krafft Studios collection; point krafftco.com/license at Watermark.
4. Sealed files bound to a recipient's email; seal licensed deliveries so they close at term end.
5. Transfers of rights (chain of title on the life page); counsel first on assignment formalities and securities questions.

- Companies (Will, 18 Oct: Natora is the first corporation licensing our photographs, with Watermark's images linked into their site): `src/clients.js`, `public/site.js`. Studio → Back office → Companies (/studio/clients): a company with its sites; 'License photographs' takes slugs or addresses (a line may add the page it hangs on), a use (default commercial) and a fee or none (part of the engagement); each is an ordinary license (licensee_company, no email, so nothing is mailed to the company from here), granted and told on the register (`client.licensed` hidden). On their site: `<script src="BASE/site.js" data-client="slug" defer>` and `data-wm` (slug, ref or number) on each img; the image is served under its license at /p/:ref/:size (counted by Referer page in `placements`, an hour's cache; 410 once ended, and the script then shows a quiet 'license has ended' card), the credit sits on it (© maker · Licensed · the Watermark number → /t/). `data-wm-keep` keeps their own file, `data-wm-credit` below|none. /api/client/:slug (CORS). /for/:slug, the public account, only when the Studio ticks it with the company's yes. Test: smoke 'companies'.
- The opening (Will, 18 Oct: a show in Meatpacking as the big launch, where photographers sign up): `src/opening.js`. A public hang with an opening date (Make it a show) carries a reply card (`#opening`, `.op`): name, email, a guest, 'I make photographs' (honeypot `.hpot`, 6 per 10 min per address). Kept in `rsvps` (one per email per show); the guest is written to; the house and the wall's curator are told. A photographer is handed the application (/join with name and email filled). The list: /hang/:id/guests and .csv (curator or Studio). Nothing on the register. Test: smoke 'galleries'.
- Bravo (Will, 18 Oct: the hat is a nod; bravo is jubilation, "this work just blew my socks off"): `bravos` table and POST /w/:slug/bravo in applause.js; three a month per person (BRAVOS_A_MONTH, a draft), taken back freely. Work page: `.bv-b` (the word Bravo! in the serif, gold when given) beside the hat, the count shown from one ('One bravo', 'N bravos'), `public/bravo.js` (the word swells, gold flecks `.bv-fx`, a haptic, 'N left this month'). Stroll rail: `[data-st-bravo]` after Hat. The maker is told at once (notice kind `bravo`, on by default); the register says 'Someone called Bravo! for …' (Applause chapter, grouped in Lately); regard weighs a bravo as five hats (cap 40). Test: smoke 'bravo'.
- The Collect glyph (Will, 18 Oct: water, and the house's own mark): `I.collect` is the Watermark wave itself (the logo's path, filled and stroked 1.4 at scale .3293) centred in the ring (stroke 1.3).
- The Collection's four doors (Will, 18 Oct: the Collection is the largest entry into the archive; it must traverse the makers' art, what curators hang, what galleries show, and the collections people keep): under the cover, `.dr4` (four dark doors, each with three of the photographs behind it fanned, a kicker, a name, a line, a count): The archive (#archive, the rooms and search, now headed 'The archive.'), Walls (#walls), Shows (#shows), Collections (#collections; `collectorCard`). An empty door leads to where the first can be made. Bands follow in that order; `publicCollections()` and `showsNow()` in app.js feed both /collection and /collectors. Phones: the doors scroll sideways, one and a bit at a time. Headers: 'For those who collect.' (Collection) and 'For those who make.' (/makers).
- In a headset (Will, 18 Oct: the biggest thing we can do; the work in beautiful grand spaces): `src/vr.js`, `public/vr.js` (three.js 0.169 from node_modules, served at /vendor/three.module.min.js, nothing from a CDN). /vr?in=<set>&space=gallery|rotunda|night&at=<slug>: the long gallery (coffered ceiling with a lantern of light, parquet, the first photograph large on the far wall, the rest facing one another), the rotunda (dome, coffers, oculus and its shaft of light, marble rings), the night (photographs alone on a spiral, each in its own pool of light). Sets are the walk's (`app.locals.walk.list`); private walls and galleries refused; up to 30 at screen size. Each photograph in a dark case with a warm wash and a museum label (title, maker, place, year). Screen: drag to look, click a photograph to stand before it, the floor to walk there, WASD/arrows. Headset (Quest, Vision Pro, any WebXR browser): 'Enter in your headset' (immersive-vr, local-floor, hand-tracking); point and select or look and pinch; a ring on the floor where a pointer lands. Permissions-Policy allows xr-spatial-tracking for self. Linked from the Collection's views ('In a headset') and every public wall ('Walk this wall in a headset'). Test: smoke 'in a headset'.
- The acts in the viewing room (Will, 18 Oct: enjoy the work first; hat and bravo; collecting the first thing one can buy and easy, never preying): one quiet row at one weight, Hat · Bravo! (B) · Hang it · On your TV (/frame) · Collect $5, Collect last and the only act with a price (gold `#d9bc7c`), 'one tap from your pocket' and the edition number in its title, not on the wall. The hat shows no count in the room. 'The photograph' sits beneath the row.
- Grand Central and the Great Hall (Will, 18 Oct: watermark.com is the entrance that orients everybody and routes them, beautifully, like Grand Central; the Collection is the museum itself, entered like the Met; the old buildings said "I love you" to the people who came in). One view, `collection({ front })`: `/` is the station: `.gh-hero` (100svh, the cover drifting from 1.09 to 1 over 28 s, 'Watermark' / 'Where great work is kept.' / count line / 'Where to?'), the welcome line, Departures (`.gc`, a board of eight lines with a thumbnail, a line and a gold track number: The collection, On view, The makers, For those who collect (/yours), For those who make (/makers), For editors and companies (/trade), Check a photograph (/verify), Simply to look (/stroll)), the information desk (`.gh-desk`: Today's photograph, On view, Find anything (GET /collection?q=, read on load into the archive search), Other ways in, Open always), then the shows, walls, collections and Lately bands. No archive grid on the front. `/collection` is the museum: the same hero ('The collection' / 'Enter the museum.'), the Great Hall (`.ghall`, three ways: the wings by place on the left, the grand staircase to the masterworks in the middle, the makers on the right), the masterworks salon (`.gh-salon`, curated and five-star first, every third hung full width), the wings (`.gh-band`, a full-bleed band per room), then the archive (search, rooms, plates). 'For those who collect.' heads Yours. All headline words are drafts for Will.
- Art, not only photographs (Will, 18 Oct: Watermark has always been about mixed media; get away from 'photography'): the entrance (front and collection) says works, not photographs ('Where great work is kept.', 'N works by M makers', Check a work, Today's work, Search N works, Every work). The rest of the house still says photograph in many places (work pages, guide, letters, Studio); sweep it page by page, keeping 'photograph' only where a work is one.
- First steps (Will, 18 Oct: everything we do for ourselves, others can do; the test is an outside maker getting from the invitation letter to a page, prices and a paid sale without help): `src/steps.js`. A card on the maker's studio (`.fs`, 'First steps · n of 7') until every step is done or the maker puts it away (POST /account/steps/away): Your page (portrait or a bio over 20 characters) · Your work (any work) · Your best in front (one published and one graded) · Your prices (opening /account/prices ticks it) · Your key (a passkey) · Getting paid (`stripe_account`) · Your page, sent (Copy your page: Web Share on a phone, else the clipboard; POST /account/steps/shared). Ticked only by what the house can see; `maker_steps` table; never shown to the Studio's view of a maker or to galleries. Test: smoke 'first steps'.
- The sweep to 'work' (18 Oct): the house's general framing now says work or works (counts, headings, buttons, the vault, hangs, keeping tiers, the stroll's description, Add work, License a work, The life of a work, bravo's title). Kept as 'photograph': lines about a specific piece (every piece held today is one: 'Photograph by …', 'License this photograph', the guide's 'from the photograph and its record'), the Copyright Office's group registration (GRPPH is for photographs), Will's own essay sentences, and delivered-file captions. When other media arrive, give works a kind and let those lines read from it.
- Collect within reach (18 Oct, found walking the live site on a phone: Collect sat three and a half screens down, under the guide and the study): the work page's act row (`.ap`) now carries Collect beside the hat and the bravo (`.ap-col`, the wave ring, 'Collect', the price in gold), at their weight; it submits the page's own Collect form (`[data-collect-now]` → `form[data-share].requestSubmit()`), so the pocket one-tap and Apple Pay hold.
