Shown under the same condition as the form. The hero's words sit on a soft shade (`.gh-hero-t:before`) so they read on a pale cover; the Departures board gives each line its own picture.
- Deploys: the service has a disk, so each deploy restarts it and the site answers 502 for about a minute (first-time visitors see Render's page; returning ones get sw.js's last copy). Batch changes into fewer pushes.
- Dark rooms to the edge (Will, 18 Oct, on the installed iPhone app: the strip under the stroll was white): `layout` marks a bare page whose main is .st, .vr, .fm or .xr with `html.dk`, and html.dk paints html and body the wall's black (#0b0a09), so the home-indicator strip matches the room.
- Paid is granted, always (18 Oct: Will's $5 Collect paid in place reached Stripe but was never granted; the page's /pay/done and the webhook both missed it): `settle(l)` in app.js asks Stripe about a license's PaymentIntent or Checkout session and grants what is paid (filling the buyer's email from the payment when the sheet asked none, so the letter goes); `settleAll()` runs 20 s after start and every 5 minutes over licenses awaiting payment from the last three days; /pay/:ref/done, the payment_intent.succeeded webhook and /w/:slug?paid=<ref> all go through it. `[settle]` in the logs.
- Send it again (Will, 18 Oct): the license page (/c/:ref) counts a signed-in holder (`buyers.id` = `licenses.buyer_id`) as the owner, settles a still-unpaid license with Stripe on view, and offers 'Send it to me' (`.pr-again`, POST /c/:ref/resend: the collector's letter to the license's address or a new one, kept when the license had none; once a minute). /healthz `mailState` (sent, failed, the last result and Resend's error, the sending domain's status) so a quiet mail failure is seen.
- The stroll's room rule is `main.st` (18 Oct: the bare `.st` also matched the studio's star grading form, `form.st`, and laid a black screen over the maker's studio).
- Mail's sender (18 Oct: no letter went out from the evening of the 17th: MAIL_FROM in Render named a krafftco.com address and Resend has verified only watermark.krafftco.com, so every send was refused 403): `send` in mail.js retries a refused sender as 'Watermark <house@watermark.krafftco.com>'. Will: set MAIL_FROM back to that, or verify krafftco.com in Resend (DNS, his).
- Letters again (18 Oct, once): `lettersAgain()` in app.js, a minute after start, sends the collector's letter for every active license granted between 17 Oct 22:30 UTC and the mail fix that has an address, then tells the house maker in one note; settings key `letters_again_1017` marks it done.
- Connect's reason shown (18 Oct: Will could not add a bank; the page only said Stripe could not be reached): a failed /account/payouts/connect keeps Stripe's own message, shows it to the maker (ok=connect) and on /healthz `connect`.
- The holder's room (Will, 18 Oct: collectors are most of the house; what they hold must be the best page, with everything to use it; print it, see it in person; turn analog; the Frick is the model): `src/keep.js`. /yours/p/:ref (holder or Studio; others go to /c/:ref): the piece on its cotton mount on a deep green wall (`.kp-wall`, `.kp-mount`, pencil line), 'X is yours.', acts (Print it · On your TV · Hang it · Save it · The papers); Print it (#print): the file (Collect = 2400 px, else the original), `sizes()` per paper (fine ≥290 ppi, good ≥230, wall ≥170), Collect the Reference to print larger (originals ≥7000 px), Print it yourself in five steps, Or have it printed (POST /yours/p/:ref/order → `print_orders`, mailed to the house and the asker; `print.asked` hidden); the prints of this piece (records via /yours/print with back=); Live with it (TV, a wall, phone, Look closer); the papers. /print: the house's guide to printing (file, paper, size, lab, frame at 57 in, record it). The gallery: cards open the holder's room; tabs are Gallery · Walls · Papers · Market · Cabinet · More (books, patronage, settings; old ?tab= still open); a band invites the first unprinted piece to be printed (`.yc-print`). docs/HOUSE.md 'Turning analog'. Test: smoke (holder's room, order, /print).
- Stars fixed (18 Oct): public/stars.js posted FormData (multipart), which the app does not parse, so every click graded 0; it now posts URLSearchParams.
- The print room's desk (18 Oct): Studio → Prints (/studio/prints, in keep.js): every print asked for, moved Asked → Being printed → Sent → Delivered (or Declined); the asker is mailed at each step. The holder's room also opens with the license key (?k=, as /c/ does), so the collector's letter carries 'Print it' (`printUrl` in letterFor) and the license page links 'Print it, hang it, live with it'. Footer: Print your work.
- One Wander button (Will, 18 Oct: 'We recommend you wander'; get people into the stroll fast, and train them to recognise the button): the same mark everywhere, the wave and the word Wander (`WAVE_MARK`, `[data-nv-wander]`), and it always opens the stroll: in the header (now /stroll on every device), as the first act in the front door's hero (`.gh-wander`, above 'Where to?') and in the line under it, 'We recommend you wander.' (`.cx-wander`, `.cx-wgo`), which replaces the old welcome; both start at the hero's work (?at=). The phone rewrite leaves links already at /stroll alone. The viewing room (/wander/room) remains for W and its own links.
- Bravo's glyph (Will, 18 Oct: an iconic gesture, a little opera man, the hat's companion, after Ask Jeeves): `I.bravo` in icons.js, a gentleman in tails with arms up, throwing his top hat in the air (its own viewBox, cropped so it sits at the hat's weight). The work page's bravo is now a round glyph button beside the hat (`.bv-b`, gold when given); the stroll rail and the viewing room show the glyph and 'Bravo' (stroll.js harvests it from the first screen like the others). The flying 'Bravo!' word on press stays.
- Find, straight to the picture (Will, 18 Oct: finding art is always hard; you want to see this picture again, fast): `src/find.js`, `public/find.js`. One search everywhere: the header's Find (`.nv-find`, `[data-find-open]`), the stroll's bar (`data-find-open="stroll"`: a work opens inside the stroll), the / key or ⌘K, and /find (the page, no script needed). GET /api/find?q= (`search`: an in-memory index of held works rebuilt each minute; tokens scored on title, place, maker, keywords, caption, year, accession, prefix and whole-word; typos forgiven by one edit with transposition, else trigrams; makers, places, rooms (public collections) and public walls alongside) and with no q (`before`: where you stood lately by `stood`, yours by active licenses, then places, makers, rooms). The overlay `.fd` lists works with pictures; ↑↓ choose, Enter opens the first (work page; the stroll at it on a phone or in the stroll), a word with no work → /stroll?q=. `app.locals.findIndexDirty()` drops the index. Test: smoke 'find'.
- Settings in the stroll (18 Oct): one sheet (`.st-ss`, `[data-st-settings]`, the `dial` glyph) replaces the bar's Sit and Guide toggles: Sit a while (the preset: 90 s, the guide read, quiet music), The guide (Off · Hints · Full), Pace (You scroll · 15 · 30 · 90 s; `wm_stroll_pace`; a timer moves on; wake lock), Sound (switches: quiet music `wm_stroll_music`; the guide read aloud `wm_stroll_guide`). Stand up sets it all back. The bar is Watermark · chip · Find · Settings · Close; the first screen on a phone keeps its bar five seconds so newcomers see them.
- Turning the phone (Will, 18 Oct: a horizontal work should be enjoyed sideways, full screen): `resnap()` in public/stroll.js holds the current work through resize/orientationchange (snap off, scrollTop to its offset, the observer ignored while turning) and sets `.st-land`; landscape on a phone shows the picture alone edge to edge (no padding), the plate small in the corner, the rail in a row, chrome only on a tap.
- Wilhelm (Will, 18 Oct: a mascot like Vonnegut's self-portrait, but chic, an artist, a character of Will, like Hitchcock's profile; named Wilhelm): `I.wilhelm` in icons.js (own viewBox 8 4 36 46; the quiff as the wave, round glasses, beard, neckerchief; drawn from Will's Photo Booth portraits in ~/Pictures, a first draft). Shown with the guide ('Look closer · read by Wilhelm', `.lc-ai`), in the stroll's guide sheet (`.st-wil`), in Find's foot and 'nothing by that word' lines (find.js, template data-ic=wilhelm), and on the signs (`.nf-wil`). docs/HOUSE.md 'Wilhelm'. Said on /print ("Or ask the house") and in the holder's room's 'Or have it printed' (keep.js). A lab for larger or framed work is still to be chosen.

POST /yours/p/:ref/proof: from the pocket in one tap when it covers (the button says so), else Stripe Checkout (metadata.proof; `app.locals.keepEvent` on checkout.session.completed; /yours/p/:ref/proof/thanks settles on return). `print_orders` gained kind ('ask'|'proof'), cents, paid, stripe_session, ship_name, address; a proof is 'pending' until paid, then 'asked' on Studio → Prints with "The proof · paid $25" and the address; the house and the collector are mailed; `proof.collected` on the register (hidden). Preview: /studio/letters?as=wall. Words a draft. Test: smoke.
- The agreements (Will, 19 Oct: get ahead of the legal challenges; make it the most robust it can be): `src/legal.js`. Six documents, each versioned (LEGAL VERSION, a date) and fingerprinted (sha256 of the plain text at /legal/<key>.txt): the terms of the house (/terms; the old plain-language license page is gone), Privacy (/privacy), the Maker Agreement (/legal/makers), the Curator and Gallery Agreement (/legal/curators), Claims and notices (/legal/claims; DMCA), Prints and proofs (/legal/prints); /legal lists them. Acceptances (`acceptances`: who maker|buyer|curator, id, doc, version, hash, ref, ip) are recorded at the moment that matters: a collector at payment (`grant` with paid; Checkout's submit text names the terms), a maker at first publication (the attestation checkbox now also accepts the agreement), a curator on sending a show (said by the button); `terms.accepted` on the register (hidden). Studio → Agreements (/studio/legal): fingerprints, counts, mark reviewed (`settings legal_reviewed_<key>`; the page drops its draft notice; `terms.reviewed`/`terms.draft` hidden). Every word is a draft for counsel; the packet of open questions is `docs/COUNSEL.md`. Footer: Terms · Privacy · The agreements. Test: smoke 'the agreements'.
- Security 2/6, backups (19 Oct): `src/backup.js`. Nightly at BACKUP_HOUR UTC (7; an hour after start if none in a day): SQLite's online backup, gzipped, into the vault (`backups/db/watermark-<stamp>.db.gz`, sealed by VAULT_KEY before it leaves the server), `backups/latest.json`, the portraits beside it (`backups/portraits/`); copies older than BACKUP_KEEP_DAYS (30) pruned, never below three. The restore drill (weekly, Sundays, and on demand): the latest copy fetched back, opened read-only, integrity_check, its register compared at the same seq with the live one and verified as a chain; a tampered copy fails. `backup_runs` keeps every run; /healthz `backup` (last, drill, stale); Studio → Back office → Backups (/studio/backups: the vault's copies, Back up now, Restore drill now, drill any copy); failures mailed to the house and `backup.failed` on the register (hidden). By hand: `node scripts/restore.js [key]` → watermark.restored.db. The store gained `del` and `size`. The old /studio/backup button remains. Test: smoke 'backups'.
- Security 1/6, the Studio door (19 Oct): `src/door.js`. STUDIO_EMAILS (Render, comma-separated) names who may enter; once set, ADMIN_PASSWORD is retired (POST /studio/login 403; GET → /studio/door). Two factors within ten minutes: the email code or its one-tap link (signin.js `admit` → `app.locals.studioFactor(…,'code')`) and the person's passkey (auth.js login verify → 'passkey'); with no passkey yet the code alone opens the door and the page asks for one. `studio_factors` holds the fresh factors; the door opens with `app.locals.openStudio(res)` (the wm_s cookie, 14 days); `studio.opened` on the register (hidden; the password path records factors 'password'). /studio/door shows the two steps and runs the passkey. /signout now clears wm_s. Will: set STUDIO_EMAILS to his address(es), add a passkey at Yours → Settings. Test: smoke 'the Studio door'.
- Security 3/6, the audit (19 Oct): a middleware on every POST /studio/* (not /login) tells `studio.action` on the register after the response: the route, the fields that matter (AUDIT_KEYS; anything named like a password, secret, token, cert or key is never recorded), the HTTP status, who (`by`: a maker handle, a masked email, or 'studio'), the address. Hidden from the public register. Studio → Back office → Audit (/studio/audit): those entries with the door's openings, reviews, backup and fixity failures, decisions, payouts. Test: smoke 'the audit'.
- The Keys (Will, 19 Oct: the house's private members' club, Krafft's, across Studios and Watermark; "the balcony lounge at the Met"; every maker, collector and curator holds keys; the Keys is where every conversation lives; nothing social on the wall): `src/keys.js`. `keys` (kind maker|collector|curator, maker_id or buyer_id, no per kind, how): earned, never bought, numbered in the order given, never reissued. Given on the register's own events (license.granted paid social/reference → collector; hang.opened → curator; work.published / maker.invited / maker.introduced → maker, a gallery → curator) and by `backfill()` at start and every ten minutes (makers by joined_at; collectors by first paid personal license; curators by archive keys and shown walls). `key.given` hidden. `app.locals.keys`: issue, holds(req) (kinds held; the Studio counts as 'house'), any, ofMaker, ofBuyer, tag/tags. Shown: /yours marks ("The Keys: the collector's key, No. 12"), the maker's dashboard (`.hd-keys`), the maker page beside the roles (`.mh-key`). /keys: the door ("Are you a maker, a collector, or a curator?"), the three rooms with counts and what each key opens, the rules of the house (nothing social on the wall; you may speak once you have stood before it; signed, a sentence at least; hours; discretion; the dinner), Friends of the house as dues not a door. Footer: The Keys. Next: the benches (src/bench.js: under works and walls, the standing rule, the guide's opening question), the balcony (hours, a host, the wall of the night, the piano, the long table, shelved at midnight), the dinner and the season. Test: smoke 'the Keys'.
- The benches (Will, 19 Oct): `src/bench.js`. Under every work (/bench/:slug) and every public wall (/bench/wall/:slug): the picture small at the top, the conversation beneath, chronological, well-seen lines first; signed (a name on the gallery or studio), a sentence at least (20 characters, a space), no links, the plain insults turned away, 6 per 10 min. Reading is open; speaking needs a key (`app.locals.keys.holds`) and, for a work, standing: BENCH_SECONDS (60) before it (`stood`, by buyer or viewer code), or holding it, or having made it, or a well-seen line anywhere (bench or guest book); on a wall any keyholder. Wilhelm opens a work's bench with the study's first question (`.bn-q`); a wall's with its line. The maker (work), the curator (wall) or the house mark 'well seen' or remove; a writer removes their own. The maker or curator is told (notice kind `bench`, who 'both'). Nothing on the register. The wall carries only the door (`bench.door`: `.bn-door`, in `.bn-sec` above the guest book on the work page and above the opening card on a wall), never a line. Test: smoke 'the bench'.
- The balcony (Will, 19 Oct: the house keeps the work; it does not keep the people; a space where talk disappears, like the world): `src/balcony.js`, `public/balcony.js`. /balcony, the Keys' room: open by hours (BALCONY_OPEN 21 to BALCONY_CLOSE 24 in BALCONY_TZ America/New_York; BALCONY_ALWAYS=1 for a dev house; the house opens or closes it by hand, POST /studio/balcony/open|close), a minute tick. Everything said lives in this process's memory only (`room`: present, lines capped at 400, words, asks) and is gone at close or restart; no model reads it; nothing is logged. SSE at /balcony/stream: keyholders inside get state, presence and lines; anyone else looks through the window (state and presence only; the piano as 'Something new, for those present'). POST /balcony/say (keyholders, room open); /balcony/word {to, body}: a word between two people present, sent only to the two, gone when either leaves; /balcony/host (a maker, curator or the house takes the chair when it is empty); /balcony/wall {slug} and /balcony/piano {slug} (host; the piano is a work of the host's own, held or not, shown to those present, Collect linked when published); /balcony/keep/:id (host asks) and /balcony/keep/:id/yes (the speaker; the line goes to the bench under the wall of the night in their name; the only talk ever kept). Presence is first names while present and nothing after. `balcony_nights` and `balcony.night` on the register (public: 'The balcony was open, X hosting; on the wall, Y; N came. Nothing said was kept.'). Privacy gained 'The balcony keeps nothing'. Next: the dinner and the season. Test: smoke 'the balcony'.
- The Keys, after the restaurant (Will, 19 Oct: "pull up the Keys menu and the Keys logo; that's the basis for the thing; my great-grandmother started this restaurant; tell that story, and the story of the name; this is the feel; bring this kind of experience to the 21st century; it becomes our digital clubhouse at Watermark"): the Keys restaurant, 1820 N. Meridian Street, Indianapolis, born 1947, kept by Mary Helen Krafft (Mrs. Wm. R.); the keys it gave guests opened the front door. Source on the Mac: `Documents/03_AREAS/02_KRAFFT/03_Ventures/THE_KEYS` (the 1968 menu scans, `the_keys.ai`, Will's vector of the matchbook). `public/keys/`: `mark.svg` (The KEYS + the keys; the word Restaurant removed, 19 Oct), `bunch.svg` (the keys on their tassel), `matchbook.svg` (with INDIANAPOLIS · close cover before striking), `damask.svg` (an original tile, drawn here), `menu-1..4.jpg`; all served under /static/keys/. keys.js inlines the marks (`MARK`, on `app.locals.keys.MARK`) so they take the page's colour. /keys opens with the mark in gold on red damask (`.ky-cover`, `.ky-damask`), a 'Keys Comments' line (`.ky-script`: Snell Roundhand / Apple Chancery / cursive, the menu's script) and a link to /keys/story: 'Born in 1947', the restaurant from the menu's own Keys Comments (the portraits by Hans Limbach, the rooms, the famous, the farm, nine Holiday awards), the four menus, The name (a bracketed blank, `.ks-blank`, for Will: the dealership, the make, the years, Ed Krafft's place in the line, from his pictures and obituaries, which are not in the connected folders), What carries over (the keys; the rooms: benches = the bar lounge, the balcony = the Champagne Room, the dinner = the Keyhole Room; the manner; the look). The balcony and the bench door wear the bunch in gold and a red rule. Colours `--kred #a90f18`, `--kgold #e0a63c`. Every word of the story a draft for Will. Test: smoke 'the Keys' (the mark, the story, the scan).

The season: `nights` (slug, kind opening|late|dinner|salon|screening|walk|party, at, city, venue, address (only to those coming), capacity, circle_first, work_slug/hang_slug for the picture, makers present, host, status draft|open|past|cancelled, came); /season (coming, held, how a night works), /season/:slug (the picture, the words, the artists, the reply, who is coming by first name and 'collects · makes · the Circle' to those coming, 'Leave your card' beside each); POST /season/:slug/reply (a key or the Circle; the Circle alone for CIRCLE_FIRST_HOURS (48) after a night opens; a guest only on the guest level; a waiting list past capacity, the first waiting takes a freed place; 'You are expected' letter), /cancel. Studio → The season (/studio/season: make/edit, guests + .csv, mark came; opening a night tells the Circle by letter and notice and `night.opened` on the register ('The house opens its doors in New York: …'); past → `night.held` with the count). The Circle: `circle` (level circle $600 | guest $1,200 a year, drafts; CIRCLE_CENTS/CIRCLE_GUEST_CENTS; attest 'I am under forty', no document, nothing else stored), /circle (velvet cover, why, what a member holds, the next nights, join → Stripe subscription by the year, metadata.circle, `app.locals.seasonEvent`; members see first names of the Circle; leave at year's end), /circle/:id/thanks (a gallery and the collector's key given), /studio/circle (the roll); `circle.joined/ended` hidden. The cards: `cards` (from_owner/to_owner 'b:<id>'|'m:<id>', about night:|work:|wall:, note, answer, introduced_at) and `meet_prefs` (open, a private line); /meet/card?to=&about= (keyholders; one a day; never twice; twelve characters, no links; the receiver told by notice kind `card` and mail), /meet (left for you: Yes, introduce us / Not now; yours, left; taking cards + your line), POST /meet/:id/answer (yes → `introduction.made` hidden, one letter to each with both addresses, the other's line and the next night; no → nothing said). `app.locals.season.cardLink(req, line, about)` puts 'Leave your card' on signed bench lines (bench.js). Names: a collector's gallery may carry no name, so `givenName` takes the one given to the Circle or a night. Keys rooms gain The Season and The Circle; the Keyhole Room points at /season; the stroll's Keys door shows the next night; footer The season · The Circle. Notify kinds night, card, circle. Studios films the nights. Every word a draft for Will. Test: smoke 'the season, the Circle and the cards'.
- The Keyhole Room (19 Oct): a night of kind `dinner` in season.js is drawn, not first come. Names go down (`night_guests.status='asked'`, 'Put my name down'; a letter says so); the house draws the table on Studio → The season → Guests ('Draw the table': the Circle first, then the order of asking, up to `capacity` or DINNER_SEATS (12); or a name at a time with Seat) → `seat()` makes them 'going' and sends 'A place at the table' with the address and the menu (`nights.menu`, shown only to the seated on the page too); the rest are written to ('kept for the next'). The Keys' Keyhole Room card points at the season. Test: smoke 'the Keyhole Room'.
- Harder to copy (Will, 19 Oct: "what can we do to make it harder to reverse engineer"; "do it"; the open API stays as it is, "whatever is best for platforms"): (1) Renditions carry the mark: `images.setMarker` (app.js, with MARK_KEY) makes every screen and large copy served at /img carry the invisible mark with the work's rendition payload (`mark.renditionPayload` = 0x80000000 | work id; `isRendition`, `renditionWork`; `embedRaw` marks the raw pixels before the JPEG/WebP encode); thumbs are left alone; marked renditions are cached as `<slug>-<size>m.<fmt>` (`images.fileOf`), so old caches rebuild and `replaceMaster` clears both names. `mark.trace` answers `{ rendition: true, work }` for one, and /verify says 'a copy taken from the house's own walls … it never left under a license; no one holds it'. (2) The mark whitened: a payload of mostly one bit (a small download id, a rendition) cancelled itself against its neighbours at read, so it could not be read back; the bits are now XORed with a key-derived pattern (`whiteBits`) at embed and read, old files read as before (the check byte decides), and the read takes the picture's global residual out and clips each block's vote (`CLIP`). (3) Scripts and styles leave stripped: `src/served.js` minifies public/*.js with terser (comments off, no toplevel mangle) and strips comments from CSS into `cache/served/` on first request (rebuilt when the source's mtime changes); the source files keep their comments; /sw.js goes the same way; sw.js `V` bumped. Trade-secret hygiene and trademarks (the wave, Watermark, the Keys) are Will's with counsel. Test: smoke 'harder to copy'.
- The desk (Will, 19 Oct: "a dashboard where I can see into everything; my own personal site; one of my own sites, separate, secret; fullerton.cloud; for Krafft; exactly like the New Yorker; a mascot-ish figure"): `desk/` in this repo, its own Node service (Express, no database), deployed by hand on Render with root directory `desk` and the custom domain fullerton.cloud (DNS Will's; `desk/render.example.yaml` lists the environment; never a blueprint). The door: DESK_EMAIL only, a six-digit code or one-tap link by Resend, a signed cookie (DESK_SECRET) for 30 days. The front, set like a magazine (cream paper, Bodoni Moda masthead 'The Desk', the date line, the figure): The House (Watermark: sales today/week/month/all, works, makers, collectors, licenses, stood, the Circle, Friends, the register's health, lately sold), The Ledger (Stripe balance and last charges by a restricted read key; owed to makers; Render services and deploys), Needs You (requests, claims, applications, prints, dinner names, feedback; Notion's open tasks from NOTION_SOURCES, Label=data_source_id), Goings On (the season's nights; Studios' latest by Ghost Content API), The Wire (the register's lately). Watermark answers `GET /api/desk` (src/desk-api.js, `x-desk-key` or ?key= must equal DESK_KEY; private, no-store) with the summary; `app.locals.deskSummary`. Sources cached a minute. The figure `desk/public/figure.svg`: an original gentleman in profile (homburg, round spectacles, a photograph held to the light, the keys on their tassel), a draft without a name. Test: `cd desk && npm test`.
- The pictures (20 Oct; Will: "this kind of stuff can't happen; it will affect trust if we ever degrade the quality of images"): the rendition mark of the 19th (±3 on 8×8 blocks) showed as mottling in smooth skies; it is off (`RENDITION_MARK=1` turns it on, and it must first pass the gate below and Will's eye). Rule: nothing is ever laid into the pixels of a served picture, and no change to `src/images.js` ships, without `test/pictures.js` passing: a smooth sky and a textured scene go through `images.derivative` as served (JPEG and WebP, screen and large) and must stay within 0.2 levels of block structure (0.6 for texture) of the resized original and above 45 dB (sky) / 38 dB (scene) PSNR; the marked pipeline is run to prove the gate catches it. Quality: JPEG 88/90 4:4:4, WebP 90/92 (`Q` in images.js); rendition file names carry `q2`, so every older copy is rebuilt; sw.js `V` bumped to `wm-2026-10-20img2` so devices drop cached marked copies (the app keeps /img cache-first). `originalPath` writes the vault copy atomically. CSS is served as written (STRIP_CSS=1 minifies); JS is minified.
- You already own it (Will, 20 Oct: "the most intuitive, simple copyright app ever … people assume if they don't register they're not copyright owners … give people rights they have never exercised"; "yes" to the open register): `src/own.js`. The deed (/w/:slug/deed: who made it, when, the fingerprint, the register entry and the house's signature, the Office's state, the credential; printable; held works public, hidden ones to the maker and the Studio); the arrival sentence (`app.locals.yours(w, m)`: "This is yours. It has been since …, when you made it. No one may use it without your yes." in the vault's answer `yours` + `deed`, shown under each arrival; `.vt-yours`); /own ('You already own it': five things people get wrong, three things you can do, the Office, the door); the open door (POST /record name + email → a maker with `makers.door='record'`, signed in by `admit`, mailed, `maker.recorded`; the collection stays curated: a recorded maker cannot publish (`app.locals.mayHang`, need=invited) until Studio → Accounts 'Invite to the house' (POST /studio/accounts/:id/invite → door 'house', `maker.invited` from 'record', mailed)); the way in (`app.locals.wayIn(m, ok)`, `.hd-way` on a recorded maker's studio: Record · Prepare (title, place, a sentence, stars) · Your page and your prices · Ask the house to look → POST /account/ask-house → `house_asks`, `house.asked`, the house mailed; Studio → Accounts 'Asking to hang' (`houseAsksBlock`): Invite, or a word back (POST /studio/accounts/:id/answer, mailed); the house has its own eye but never tells an artist what to make: craft, honesty, a body of work); the Office one tap (/account/copyright: the maker's works by state, tick → POST /account/copyright/ask groups them the Office's way (published/unpublished × year, 750 a group) → Checkout at the Office's fee per group (COPYRIGHT_FEE_CENTS 5500, at cost; metadata.copyright_ask; `app.locals.ownEvent`), `copyright_asks` paid → 'asked', `copyright.asked`, the house and the maker mailed; `app.locals.copyrightAsks()` for Studio → Copyright; POST /studio/copyright/asks/:id/filed); said simply (`app.locals.saidSimply(m, uses)`: "<First> says: you may use this in a story for $350, for a company for $750 or on a wall for $250; for before an audience, ask." on /account/prices (`.own-say`) and the /license page). Shield's Authorship row links the deed; footer 'You already own it'. Words drafts; counsel reads the deed and /own before launch.
- The rooms (Will, 20 Oct: child-friendly and safe by default, "trust your kid walking around the Met"; real art not for children hangs with its label in a room you enter knowing): `src/zone.js`. `works.zone` 'all'|'mature' + `zone_note` (set on the work's record by the maker, POST /account/works/:slug/zone, or the house, /studio/works/:slug/zone; `zone.block` on `workTerms`). The visitor's `wm_zone` cookie (180 d): 'all' (default: mature work veiled and left out of the walks; each work asks at its door), 'mature' (the adult rooms open; labels stay), 'family' (doors closed on the device; a latch, not a lock, said so); /zone chooses; POST /zone/enter opens the rooms from a door. The door: a middleware on GET /w/:slug and /m/:maker/:slug sends the sign (`.zn-door`) unless the rooms are open, the maker or the Studio. The veil: /img/:slug/:size of a mature work serves a deep blur (`<slug>-<size>-veil.jpg`, `private`, `Vary: Cookie`) unless open, so every listing and feed is safe. The walks: the stroll's feed, Wander, today's and Find leave mature work out unless open (`zone.sql(req)`); `zone.label(w)` on the work page. The house refuses what is cheap, crude or offensive at the door; the adult rooms hold only work it would hang.

- Ready to hang (Will, 20 Oct: the stock agencies once made a career possible; Watermark is the 21st-century version: "when your work is ready to be shown, when people will pay you for it, that's when you're ready for Watermark; you can put it in beforehand, but there is a moment when it is worth presenting"; the house must not become "a tranche of shit", it has to hold up as a museum and wandering has to be through great work; the record is the frame on your own wall, celebrated, and the house teaches a discipline: see your work as worth making): `src/ready.js`. Two floors: the record (open, free, deeded, nothing shown or sold) and the house (by invitation). The moment is read from the work, not asked for: `readiness(m)` (`app.locals.readiness`) = Record (READY_WORKS 12 recorded) · The edit (READY_BEST 8 graded 4–5 stars, each with title, place, date and a sentence) · The files (the edit at READY_MP 12 MP+) · Your page and your prices; the way in (`wayIn` in own.js) shows the five steps with real numbers and opens 'Ask the house to look' only when all are done; POST /account/ask-house refuses otherwise (ok=notyet). The review room /studio/review/:id (Studio → Accounts → Asking to hang → Look): the maker's edit large with grade and MP, the bio, the steps, the cameras; 'Invite to the house and hang the first show' (POST /studio/accounts/:id/invite with `hang` slugs: those publish, `work.published` by:'house' show:'first'; the rest stays in the record until the maker publishes); or 'Not yet', a word back (the answer route). The edit wanders: `app.locals.editSql(alias)` adds `(stars>=3 OR curated=1)` to /wander and the stroll's open feed (not a word, a maker or a set) once the house holds WANDER_FLOOR (40) such works; today's already had its own floor. /makers 'Two floors' (`.h-two`) says it, with 'Begin the record' and the application. All numbers drafts. Test: smoke (the ask refused, the way in opening, the review room, the first show hung).
- The committees (Will, 20 Oct: "who hangs and how it's decided: that's where a real company needs to be built; one man in one chat is not an institution"; two committees, named from the house's own story, names drafts): `src/committee.js`. The Front Door (who hangs) and the Walls (what stays), `COMMITTEES`; seats (`reviewers`: committee, name, email, label, token) given by letter from Studio → Back office → The committees (/studio/committee; POST /studio/committee/seat → mail /committee/join/:token, which signs the person in as themselves via `admit`; take back the seat) and found by the signed-in email (`reviewerOf`, `sits`). The desk /committee (seats and the Studio): the Front Door's asks waiting with votes so far → /committee/review/:id (the review room with the vote: hang with the ticked pieces, or not yet with a word); the Walls' works hung in the last thirty days with Back to the record / Not for children and a word. COMMITTEE_NEEDED (2) votes decide: hang → `app.locals.inviteMaker(m, union of the ticked, {by:'committee', seats})`; not yet → `app.locals.answerAsk` with the words joined; down → `works.status='hidden'` (never deleted, still deeded), `work.unhung`, the maker told by notice and mail; mature → zone, `work.roomed`. `inviteMaker` and `answerAsk` (own.js) are shared with the Studio's own routes. The director's say (`settings.committee_veto`, on by default for the first season): the Studio's decision stands alone and at once. The register names the seats ('X was invited to hang by Ada and Bo of the Front Door'); `committee.voted/seated`, `house.asked/answered` hidden. /hanging: the standard as the public reads it (craft, honesty, a body of work; the moment; the two committees with who sits; the register; the director's say while on); footer 'Who hangs'. Test: smoke 'the committees'.
- Wilhelm at the door (Will, 20 Oct: "we do want to efficiently route people"; "everyone likes a good tour guide, who is ours": Wilhelm; a Track/Gate suggestion from a friend declined: the board was the problem, not the word): the front door's Departures board is gone; `.gc` is now Wilhelm (`I.wilhelm`, `.gc-wil`) asking 'Who are you, today?' with three doors by who you are (`.gc-doors`: I came to look → the stroll; I collect → /yours; I make things → /makers#ready), each with a picture, a line and an act, and the rest by name in a row beneath (`.gc-ways`: The collection, On view, The makers, Editors and companies, Check a work, Find). No numbers. Phone: the doors stack; checked at 390px.
- The vault and the jewel box (Will, 20 Oct: "literally the Frick, the digital Met"; "we are the vault"; "our images need to be a one-to-one of what the artist gave us"; "if it's not beautiful, don't show it"; a digital restoration room; redundant, backed up, safe; millions of assets): docs/HOUSE.md 'The vault and the jewel box'. (1) Colour is managed: sharp converts a tagged original (Display P3, Adobe RGB) to sRGB in both formats; test/pictures.js proves it with a P3 red. (2) `wall` size 3840 (`SIZES`; made on demand, not warmed; `WARM` = thumb/screen/large): viewer.js swaps the lifted picture to /wall when the screen's pixels exceed 2400 (5K Macs, phones sideways). (3) Honest caching: /img sends `max-age=3600, must-revalidate` (was a day + a week stale); sw.js images are stale-while-revalidate inside the worker (the cached copy shows, the network copy replaces it); `V` = wm-2026-10-20img3. (4) `src/pictures.js`: the judge in the house (`judge(original, rendition, width)` → ok, soft, blocks, db, texture; `decodes(original)` with failOn truncated). (5) `src/conservation.js`, the restoration room: `conditions` (per work: decode, fixity, dims, renders, findings), `check(w)`, `condition(n)` (CONDITION_N 12 every CONDITION_EVERY_MIN 30, oldest first; mounted before /w so its sign wins); a failing held work → `works.status='restoring'` (`status_before` kept, `condition='unsound'`), `work.conserving` on the register (public: 'X by M is off view for conservation'), the maker told, the house mailed; its page is the sign `.cv-sign` 'Off view for conservation' with the way on; every public query selects 'held' so it leaves the walks and the API by itself. Studio → Back office → Restoration (/studio/restoration): the room (findings; Rebuild the display copies → `rebuild` removes every rendition and warms again, then checks; Fetch the original from the mirror; Ask the maker for the master; Check again; Back on the walls (only after a passing check, `work.restored`); Retire to the record), recent checks, Check N now, Mirror ten now. The mirror: S3_MIRROR_BUCKET/ENDPOINT/REGION + S3_MIRROR_ACCESS_KEY_ID/SECRET_ACCESS_KEY (its own client, sealed by VAULT_KEY), `mirrors` table, `mirrorSome(10)` every ten minutes, newest first; off until Will sets it (a second provider or region). /healthz `conservation`. The judge's conservation verdict fails only on PSNR under the floor or a grid of 2 levels+; a mild grid over the gate's limit is `soft` (a maker's own lower-quality JPEG re-encodes with some), so sound work is never pulled for its own file. Test: test/pictures.js (colour, the judge, a broken original) and smoke 'the restoration room'.
- The physical work (Will, 20 Oct: "not about photography … photographs of live paintings … masters, originals … the fingerprint of the physical work … collectors keep track of their collections … the ultimate tool for collectors, artists and curators, every media type"): `src/object.js`. `works.kind` (photograph default; painting, drawing, print, sculpture, textile, mixed, film, other; `KINDS`), set for a batch in the vault (`#aKind` → `registerWork({ kind })`; a photograph's medium defaults to 'Digital photograph', others keep what is given) and on the work's record ('The object', `OB.block`, POST /account|studio/works/:slug/object: kind, medium, dimensions, edition, made, signed, condition, and private to maker and house: where it is, a note; `work.object` hidden). By-lines follow the kind (`by(w, name)` 'Painting by …', `aBy` 'A painting by …', `kindWord`): the plate's credit, captions, the license page, the holder's room, the delivered file's caption (app.js `captionOf`), the API (`kind`, `medium`, `object`, `byline`). A physical work's page shows `.ob-label` (medium · dims · edition · The object) under the plate; the Shield gains 'The object' (the held file is the photograph of record); /w/:slug/object is the label and certificate (what the house attests: the photograph of record's fingerprint and register entry, the deed, the maker's record; and what it does not: the house does not hold the object, title passes outside, a Collect is a licence to the photograph of record). The cabinet as a registrar's book: columns acquired, from_whom, paid_cents, value_cents, located, condition, provenance, work_id (a held work this is the physical edition of; the maker and the deed shown), accession; the card under each cabinet work (`app.locals.cabinetRecord`, POST /cabinet/:id/record); /yours/cabinet/book (the catalogue, totals insured and paid) and /yours/cabinet.csv; private, never on the register. Test: smoke 'the physical work'.
- The easel (Will, 20 Oct: people sit in front of work in a museum and draw, paint, sculpt, the master study; tap and wander into a focus mode: pinch, scroll, zoom, hold one spot; Watermark should help people learn to draw and paint): `src/easel.js`, `public/easel.js`. /easel/:slug (held works; a mature work sends to its door; ?back=): the work alone on the dark wall (`.ez`, html.dk), the wall copy on screens that can use it; pinch, drag, wheel, double-tap, + −, arrows; Hold (L) locks the view; wake lock. The teacher's tools, CSS/SVG filters on the picture, nothing sent anywhere: Grid (G: thirds → 6×4 → off), Mirror (M), Values (V, greyscale), Squint (S, blur), Notan (N: two values → five, SVG feComponentTransfer discrete), Timer (T: 5/15/30/60 min, a three-note chime by Web Audio and a haptic), Fit (0), Study (?): Wilhelm's three studies (values in five minutes; the gesture in two; the copy in an hour) and the guide's 'What to look for'. Doors: the stroll's rail (`[data-st-easel]` 'Study', back to the stroll at the work), the viewer's deck (`G.easel`, the wilhelm glyph template), the work page's act row (`.ap-study`). Test: smoke 'the easel'.
- The house's portrait (Will, 20 Oct: "this needs to be my portrait on Krafft and Watermark right now"): `public/house/portrait.jpg` (the tweed portrait, glasses in hand, 1990×2576) ships with the code; `app.locals.seedPortrait` (accounts.js, 3 s after start) sets it as the house maker's portrait when none is set (the square 720 and `<name>-tall.jpg` at 1600). Every portrait uploaded at /account/profile now keeps its whole shape too (`-tall.jpg`); /portrait/:handle?tall=1 serves it; /director stands in the tall one (`.dr-por`, object-position 50% 20%). Replace the file to change the house's portrait; a portrait set in the studio wins.
- The Club (Will, 20 Oct: "call it the Club instead of the Circle"; the Keys is the members' club of the house, and its young members are simply the Club): every public word 'the Circle' is now 'the Club' (season.js, keys.js, the stroll's Keys door, the footer, notify's label, HOUSE.md); the door is /club (join at /club/join, thanks at /club/:id/thanks, leave at /club/end) and /circle, /circle/* redirect 301. Tables, keys and metadata keep `circle` (the `circle` table, `circle_first`, notify kind `circle`, metadata.circle, /studio/circle), so nothing paid or recorded moves. Test: smoke 'the season, the Club and the cards'.
- The front doors (Will, 20 Oct: "the staircase and the sunset over Florence are both corrupted files, and they look awful"): the files decode whole and the pixels are clean; what looks awful is size. Every one of the 366 held works arrived as a web export (303 under 4 MP, none over 12 MP: the Staircase 2400 px, Sunset over the City 2200 px), and the front door's hero stretched them to 100svh on a 5K screen with a 9% drift on top. Now: `coverFor` weighs the largest masters first (≥3840 px ×4, ≥3000 ×2), the hero carries srcset large/wall, and a master under 3000 px hangs still (`.gh-small`, no drift). The real fix is the masters: Studio → Masters, 'Choose your exports folder' (full-resolution exports; matched to each work by dhash and swapped in as the original; private archive rooms for the rest). The Europe exports are not in the Mac's connected folders (Journey Out East there holds only USA/Boston and Newport; Lightroom's catalog has no JOE files; the 2025 archive root is /Volumes/WRKF_LaCie/00-ARCHIVE/2025), so Will exports them full size or points the desk at the drive.
- The numbers (Will, 20 Oct: "cold, hard analytics: who's on the site, how things are running, what the health of things is"): `src/analytics.js`, Studio → Back office → The numbers (/studio/analytics; ?span=today|7d|30d; refreshes itself every 30 s via ?frag=1; /studio/analytics.json). First-party, nothing sent anywhere, nothing that names a person: a middleware (mounted right after `studioOnly`, before every route) records each page and API hit in `hits` (at, path folded (`page`: /w/:slug kept, ids and tokens → …), kind, status, ms, visitor (`wm_v` else a daily-salted hash of address+agent; never the address), referrer host, device, browser, bot, country when an edge header says, bytes), batched every 5 s; images and static files counted in memory only; pruned after HITS_KEEP_DAYS (90). The room: On the site now (five minutes, last hour, a minute-by-minute bar, where they are), the span against the span before (visitors, views, stood, hats, bravos, collects and $, asked, sign-ins, guest-book lines, works arrived, API), hours or days, works most seen, works longest stood before, pages, where from, devices, browsers, countries (once a CDN), speed (p50/p95, slowest pages), errors (by status, the last forty in memory), bots, Health (uptime, memory, load, register, db and disk, mail, Stripe, guides, voice, signing, backups, fixity, conservation, waiting). `app.locals.analytics` {report, health, present, flush}. Test: smoke 'the numbers'.
- The Studio's Back office menu (20 Oct): it sat inside the rooms nav, which scrolls sideways (overflow-x:auto) and clipped the dropdown, so the button looked dead; it is now a sibling of the nav in `.dk-row`.
- Large uploads, and the wall that emptied (Will, 20 Oct: "we have to learn how to deal with large uploads"; at 5:24 PM eighty works left the walls in one minute, 'off view for conservation', while the first 60 MP masters were going up; the last backup had failed on the 19th and none since): (1) Conservation never pulls a work for the house's own hiccup: a vault fetch that fails, a local copy cut short or not matching the fingerprint (dropped and fetched again), or display copies that cannot be remade are `transient` findings, noted and retried; a bad display copy is remade from the original on the spot and only reported if it fails again; off view needs a hard finding (the original itself does not decode, or its bytes are wrong in the vault) on two checks in a row; a restoring work goes back on view by itself the moment a check passes, `condition()` checks restoring works first, and a recovery pass 90 s after start re-checks every restoring work. (2) The lane: /studio/masters/one and /studio/works/:slug/master run one at a time (`inLane` in app.js) and the Masters desk sends one file at a time; `images.inspect` hashes by stream and decodes by path. (3) The originals cache: with an S3 vault the local copies are kept under ORIGINALS_CACHE_GB (3; `trimOriginals`, least recently used first; `originalPath` touches on use); stale incoming/ temp files older than a day are dropped hourly. (4) Backups: due when there is no good copy, when the last try failed over an hour ago, or past BACKUP_HOUR with the last good copy over twenty hours old; first look five minutes after start (`lastGood`). Render starter: 512 MB, 0.5 CPU, a 10 GB disk; the vault is S3. Test: smoke 'the restoration room' (a damaged display copy is remade and the work stays; a damaged original is caught on the second check; back by itself once rebuilt). The finding on all two hundred (from the house's mail): 'screenq2.jpg: blocks 1.18 (limit 0.6), 37.4 dB (floor 38)': the house's own JPEG of a detailed scene, a hair under a floor meant for the pipeline test. Conservation now judges a copy unsound only under CONDITION_DB (30 dB) or at CONDITION_BLOCKS (2 levels) and above (`sound(j)`); the strict gate stays in test/pictures.js. The house's conservation letters are a digest: one letter a minute at most for the batch (`sendDigest`), never one a work (the 200 letters reached 80% of Resend's daily quota, with the collectors' letters behind them).
- Arrivals (Will, 20 Oct: "the most efficient best app that we can, to replace Dropbox Transfer and ShootProof for creatives"): `src/arrivals.js`, `public/arrivals.js`, docs/ARRIVALS.md. The Masters desk now puts each file straight into the vault's bucket in parts (ARRIVAL_PART_MB 8, four in flight, resumable: parts done are kept in localStorage) by addresses the house signs (`store.multipart` in storage.js: start/partUrl/complete/abort via @aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner), then tells the house; a queue (`pump`, one at a time, after `app.locals.takeMaster`: the one-file logic shared with /studio/masters/one) downloads the arrival (`store.download`), takes it in (master | archive | known | smaller) and deletes it from the bucket. Table `arrivals`; routes /studio/arrivals/start, /:id/urls, /:id/done, /:id/body (a local vault takes the whole file in one streamed put: dev and the test house), /:id/abort, /:id/retry, /state (the desk polls it every 3 s). Stale uploads swept after a day; a restart puts 'working' rows back in the queue. The bucket needs a CORS rule with ExposeHeaders ETag (docs/ARRIVALS.md; Will's, once); the CSP's connect-src allows *.amazonaws.com when S3_BUCKET is set and the S3_ENDPOINT host. Next: makers' own vault through the same door; the house pulling from a watched Dropbox folder; a desktop uploader or Lightroom plugin. Test: smoke (an arrival started, put, taken in by the queue; a stranger refused).
- The makers' own vault through arrivals (Will, 20 Oct: "the makers' own vault"; "get this ready for others"): the same door for every maker. `mountArrivals` mounts the handlers twice, /studio/arrivals (purpose 'master', the Studio) and /account/arrivals (purpose 'work', the signed-in maker; a maker sees and touches only their own rows: `mine`); `arrivals` gained purpose, title, place, kind, maker_id, result (the taken-in answer as JSON). The queue calls `app.locals.takeWork(file, arrival)` (accounts.js: the one-file logic of /account/works/one, shared: registerWork as the maker's, hidden, credit, the deed line, accession, MP, fingerprint, register entry) for a work and `takeMaster` for a master. `public/arrivals.js` now exposes `window.WMArrivals` (send(file, {base, meta, onBytes, stopped}) → the arrival's id; resumable per part; a local vault takes one streamed put) and the masters desk uses it; the vault page (/account/upload, account-views.js) sends each file through it (a thin progress bar per file, 'Sent' as the first step) and then follows `/account/arrivals/state?ids=` every two seconds until the house has read each one, showing the deed line, accession and entry as before. Test: smoke (a maker's arrival taken in as theirs with place and kind; a stranger refused; the masters desk blind to it).
- The editions room (Will, 20 Oct: "we also want our art to be sold"; "perfectly with actual prints and real shows, like a curator's best friend"; the first showing is on Watermark and at the Leica gallery in Meatpacking): `src/editions.js`. `print_editions` per work (label, long_in, paper, edition_n, price_cents, framed_cents, active; EDITION_DEFAULTS 11 × 14 $250 · 16 × 24 $650 · 24 × 36 $1,400, editions of 12/12/8, framed +$150/$300/$450, drafts), opened and edited on the work's record ('The print editions', `app.locals.editionsBlock`; POST /studio|account/works/:slug/editions: open=1 adds the house's sizes, new=1 a size, id=… edits one). Sold only when the master meets the print standard (24 MP) and 'On a wall' is listed (`canSell`). The work page's `.pe` block (`app.locals.editions.block`): sizes with price and what remains, framed, 'Take it home from the show' when the visitor came from a show (`viaShow`: ?show=<hang slug> on a work page or the wm_via cookie; `app.locals.rememberShow` is mounted in app.js before the /w routes) else shipped → POST /w/:slug/print → a 'print' license (price = size + framed; rights.print {edition, label, size, paper, edition_n, framed, pickup, show}) → Checkout with the address (unless pickup) and phone → on payment `app.locals.onGrant` (called inside `grant`, which now receives `session` from the webhook and settle): the edition number allotted (never reused), a `print_orders` row kind 'edition' (size, paper, framed, pickup, show_id, work_id, edition_no, the address, 'asked' for Studio → Prints, which labels it 'The print, No. n'), `print.sold` on the register ('A print of X by M, 16 × 24 in, No. 1 of 12, was acquired at the show'), the maker told. The show (a public hang with an opening): /hang/:id/labels (curator or Studio; 5.5 × 3.4 in wall labels, two a sheet, the QR to /w/:slug?show=<slug> so each sale is the show's, the editions and prices, sold dots), /hang/:id/sales (+ .csv, the packing list: what sold, who, pickup or ship, totals), both linked from the wall's owner desk; the hang page shows 'Prints from $… · N remain' under each work of a show (`app.locals.editionLine`). `work.editions` hidden. Test: smoke 'the editions room'.
- The easel, just the picture (Will, 20 Oct: the sticky tools at the bottom are distracting): Hide (H or Tab, `[data-ez-hide]`) puts the tools and the top bar away (`.ez-hidden`), a ghost in the corner (`.ez-peek`, the dial) or H/Tab/Esc brings them back; after eight seconds still the chrome fades to near nothing (`.ez-still`) and any movement returns it.
- Wilhelm's brief (Will, 20 Oct, in pencil: "Einsteiny · AL / A.I. · Wilhelm is zany, curious, your pal, nutty professor; here to be helpful, teach you things, and be Wilhelm"; the sketch: hair up and out, bright eyes, a beard, a bow tie and jacket): docs/HOUSE.md 'Wilhelm'. The glyph is to be redrawn to it; the voice of the guide, the study, the easel and the signs is his. A first redraw to the brief (20 Oct) was frightening at glyph size and was put back; the chic draft stays until a proper pass is drawn large (several sketches for Will to choose from), then reduced. sw.js `V` = wm-2026-10-20ez2.
- The voice, never the machine's (Will, 20 Oct: "a guide keeps going off and it sounds super AI; make it elegant and beautiful"): the browser's speechSynthesis is never used anywhere (closer.js, stroll.js, room.js, viewer.js set it to null): the house's recording plays when there is one, else the guide is read with the eyes and the Hear it button stays away. The stroll's 'guide read aloud' setting lives in sessionStorage now (this visit only), so the guide never starts talking by itself on a later visit. /healthz said `speech: false`: no provider is set in Render, which is why every reading was the browser's robot. speech.js prefers ELEVENLABS_API_KEY over OPENAI_API_KEY and defaults the ElevenLabs voice to George (JBFqnCBsd6RMkjVDRZzb, a warm British narrator) unless SPEECH_VOICE names another; `fill()` records every guide once the key is set.
- The desk tells you (Will, 20 Oct: "I just put my vault in and I don't know if it's going or not"): arrivals.js says, on choosing, 'N files chosen, X GB. Nothing has been sent yet: press Send.'; while sending, 'Sending 3 of 112 · 1.1 GB of 3.4 GB · 12 MB/s · about 4 min left · <file>' in bold with the bar; at the end 'N in the vault. The house is taking them in below; you can leave this page.'; a bucket that refuses the browser is named in plain words ('the bucket needs its CORS rule (ExposeHeaders ETag)') instead of 'Failed to fetch'.
- Batch (Will, 20 Oct: "going in one by one is not acceptable"): `src/batch.js`, `public/batch.js`. Tick works on the maker's studio (/account, a box before each row, `form="batchForm"` so the per-row forms stay valid) or Studio → Works (list and wall) and a sticky bar rises (`.bt-bar`): N selected · All · None · Publish (one attestation for the lot, asked on the first press) · Make private · Stars (5 sets curated) · Place · the Studio also Curated / Not curated; shift-click ticks a run. POST /account/works/batch (the maker's own works only; the door `mayHang` and standing respected; `work.published` with batch:true for each; the Maker Agreement accepted once) and POST /studio/works/batch (back= returns to the view). The answer: '12 published; 2 not: the house has not yet invited you to hang.' Test: smoke 'batch'.
- Scripts carry the build (20 Oct, the easel's Hide "still can't" on Will's phone after three deploys): the app's static cache is stale-while-revalidate, and a background refresh that lands in a deploy's 502 minute keeps the stale copy another load; easel.js, arrivals.js and batch.js are now referenced with `?v=${VER}` (the commit), as pay.js was, so a new deploy is a new address and the old copy is never served to the new page.
- Owners (Will, 20 Oct: "one company that people can invest in", Krafft after Berkshire Hathaway, built like Hillwood; "let's make an investors page"): `src/owners.js`, /owners (/investors → 301). How Krafft, the company that owns Watermark, is owned and run: the companies with the house's live numbers; Class A owner shares (ten votes, in the founder's trust, convert to B one way only) and Class B (one vote each, the same profit, anyone may hold); no dividend while a dollar kept earns more; the owner's manual (`MANUAL`, nine principles incl. 'We love our owners': owners are the house's fourth constituency); the letter and the meeting (a Keys night); what is never touched (client money); by letter. It describes and offers nothing, said plainly: no share is sold on the site. Footer 'Owners'. Company papers for Will and counsel live in the Krafft project ('Krafft: one company', 'Counsel Packet'). Every word a draft. Test: smoke 'owners'.
- Instax (Will, 20 Oct: "print your collection into Instax", he has a Leica Sofort 2): `instaxFile` and `INSTAX` in keep.js; GET /yours/p/:ref/instax?film=mini|square|wide&mode=fill|fit (holder or Studio; live license; never counted against downloads; `print.instax` hidden). The exact pixels the printer lays down (mini 800 × 600 on 62 × 46 mm, square 800 × 800, wide 1260 × 840; portrait works turned), from the original: crop by attention or fit on white, Lanczos once, the house's recipe for instant film (gamma 1.08 shadow lift, saturation +6%, brightness +2%, a small-print sharpen), sRGB JPEG q96, named 'Title, Maker (Instax mini).jpg'. The card `.kp-instax` in the holder's room's Print it, with the four steps for Leica FOTOS. Second recipe (Will, 20 Oct, two prints in hand: 'a little flat', 'lacking depth of darkness'): `LOOKS` film | rich | plain (?look=): contrast by `linear` about a pivot above the middle (136) so the shadows take the stretch and the highlights are held, the black point pulled down 8 / 14 levels, saturation +10 / +14%, a hair cooler by `recomb`; the first recipe's shadow lift is gone. Tune by eye: print the same piece in Film and Rich beside each other. Test: smoke (holder's room).
- The house's share, paid by the collector (Will, 20 Oct: "instead of charging the creator we charge the collector, like Airbnb"; the Collect was first $7.11 ("5 to the creator, 2 to the house; 7 is my favourite number"), then settled at $7; "is this how Sotheby's would do it": yes, the buyer's premium on the hammer price, with the schedule published and the invoice itemised): `src/fee.js`. `FEE_SIDE=collector` (Render) turns it on; unset, the old way (the house keeps 100 − share_pct). Settled (Will, 20 Oct, 'make it whatever is best'; the aim is a share so plainly fair no one objects, on a volume that makes it enormous, never a hidden one): the Collect is $7 ($5 + COLLECT_HOUSE_CENTS 200; a round number reads as a price, cents as a tax); every other use and every print carries a marginal schedule like an auction house's premium, HOUSE_TIERS '20:500000,15:5000000,10' (20% of the maker's price to $5,000, 15% of the next to $50,000, 10% above: $350 → $420, $20,000 → $23,250, $100,000 → $112,750; `premium`, `schedule`, `TIERS`; `makerFromTotal` inverts by bisection). /share publishes the schedule (owners.js; footer 'The house's share'; the receipt's house line links it): what the maker receives, what the collector pays on top, what the share pays for (the card fees out of the house's share, never the maker's), what is never touched. On: `priced()` in app.js gives every use `maker_cents` (the maker's price, after the ladder), `house_cents` (Collect flat; the rest by the schedule, to the cent) and `price_cents` = the total the collector pays, so every wall, Checkout, pocket spend, PaymentIntent, basket, frame, API and eye carry the total by themselves. `licenses.house_cents` records the house's part at the sale (`stampHouse` after each insert; editions in editions.js; a request's payment link charges `FEE.total` of the fee the maker named and `grant` records the difference; with nothing recorded, `grant` applies the rule in reverse, `makerFromTotal`). The maker's side everywhere is `FEE.makerOf(l, pct)` / `FEE.sqlMaker(pct)` (price − house_cents, else share_pct%): payouts' statement, the dashboard's earned/ttm, requests' month, splits, the representation cut (`cut(l, w, pct, makerCents)`), the letters (`house` in letterFor: 'the price in full; the collector paid $7.11, of which $2.11 was the house's share'), the receipt on /c/:ref ('$5 to the maker · $2.11 the house's share'), the eye, the ledger entry (`house`). Copy with the fee on: 'The price is the photographer's; the house's share is on top' (the work page, the basket, the home page's 100%, /makers, the maker page, first steps, payouts, editions' fine print, said simply). Never 'a service fee'. Counsel: the buyer's premium's legal character and sales tax; the contract's 'License Fee' is the total paid. Test: `test/fee.js` (in npm test), and the smoke suite with the fee off.
- The rights desk (Will, 20 Oct, with a great museum's rights page in hand: a request to reproduce a work goes to one agency for North America and another for the rest of the world, archival audio to a third address, text permissions to a fourth, film stills nowhere, and the Archives' permission form is a PDF to print, sign and post; "let's solve this for museums; let's become who they want to do their digital archives with, or have us help them integrate"): `src/rights.js`. One door per institution, /rights/<handle> (makers of kind 'institution'): a held work found by word → its page and /license (the institution's own prices; a public-domain work free with the credit line); everything else (archival material, installation views, text, film, a work not yet held) asked for through one form carrying every field of the museum's own (what, the use, the publication: author/title/publisher/date, territory, deadline, name, email, affiliation, address, phone, a note) with the conditions (`makers.rights_terms`, DEFAULT_TERMS after what archives commonly require: no misrepresentation, cite as asked, no PII of living persons, the law is yours, further permissions yours, send a copy) accepted by a tick, never a signature; a honeypot; 6 per 10 min. A ticket RR-YYYY-NNNNN (`rights_requests`), mailed to the institution's rights address (`makers.rights_email`, else its email, else the house) and to the asker; the asker follows it at /rights/r/<ticket>. The institution's desk /account/rights (rail 'Rights desk' via `V.setDeskInstitution`): settings (the address, a line on the door `rights_note`, the conditions, the door open `rights_open`), the queue; /account/rights/<ticket>: grant | answer only | decline, in writing, with a fee if any; the asker mailed; a grant is the permission in writing (the page and the letter, under the conditions accepted). Studio → Back office → Rights desks (/studio/rights): every desk and request. Nothing on the register. /museums leads with 'One door, not four addresses' and 'Three ways in' (the desk only; the manifest; the platform). Words drafts; the conditions for counsel. Test: smoke 'the rights desk'.
- The Met, in (Will, 20 Oct: "the Met? I know people there"; "yes lets do it"): `parseMet` in src/iiif.js reads the Met's Open Access API (a search address → objectIDs → each object; only isPublicDomain with a primaryImage; title, artist, date, medium, dimensions, credit line, department, accession, culture, place; CC0 by default) through the same door as a IIIF manifest (Studio → Collections in). /museums/desk: the guide for a museum's rights staff (what the desk replaces, what it does, cost, the afternoon it takes). Test: smoke 'the Met, in'.
- The team (Will, 21 Oct: "every museum in the world… their own pages, their own collections, their own curators; it works for their entire team; it plugs into their archives; an online business"; "the Met and the Frick… Bloomberg Connects doesn't do that good a job… the museum's favourite partner; more powerful because everybody uses it"): `src/institutions.js`. An institution invites its team by letter from /account/staff (`institution_staff`: name, email, role director | curator | rights | registrar | shop | editor); /staff/join/:token signs the person in as themselves (`admit`) and sets `wm_staff`, and `makerOf` (accounts.js) then answers the institution for them (`actingFor`), so the studio, vault, rights desk, curators' keys and getting paid all work for the team; /staff/as/:handle chooses a desk, POST /staff/off steps back; the seat is taken back on the desk. Roles recorded and shown; finer doors by role come with the first museum's wishes. The maker page of an institution carries `museumStrip` (`.mu-strip`: works in the collection and open access, On view, Rights and reproductions, Stroll the collection, Visit; the team by role). `staff.*` hidden on the register. The maker page's split line now reads from FEE (the hardcoded 85% is gone). Test: smoke 'the team'.
- Tours (Will, 21 Oct: a tour guide like the Met's own on Instagram; "tour guides should be able to curate things they want in collections, then create recordings and walk people through; they can add notes"; "people must feel they can make a career on this platform and use their gifting… elevates people and allows people to be seen, or at least heard; the work needs to be heard"): `src/tours.js`, `public/tour.js`. A tour is a wall (hang) the curator walks you through in their own voice: on their wall's page the curator records a word on each work and a welcome (`tour_voices`: hang, work (0 = welcome), the file in the vault `tours/`, mime, seconds; the browser's MediaRecorder, three minutes a stop, 12 MB, never transcribed, no model in it; POST /tour/:id/voice/:slug|welcome, /remove); any recording makes the wall a tour. /tour/:slug (`.tr`, dark): the welcome (title, guide, line, Begin), then each stop: the photograph alone, the plate, the guide's line beneath, the recording playing on arrival (after the first tap), walking on when it ends; back/next/play, keys, swipes; GET /tour/:id/voice/:vid serves the recording with ranges (public walls cached an hour). The wall sets `wm_via`, so a Collect within thirty days pays the guide the curation share; cards as for any curator. /guides: the guides of the house (anyone whose public wall carries their voice; name, line, tours). The wall's page shows 'Take the tour, in <first>'s voice'. `tour.opened` public ('X recorded a tour of Y, in their own voice'), once per wall. Test: smoke 'tours'.
- The rooms, scoped (Will, 21 Oct: "the balcony is for people who have just looked at the Met's collection, not the Brooklyn Museum"; "a context reference, like a game's lobby size"): `src/balcony.js` keeps a room per scope (`rooms` map: 'house' or 'm:<handle>'); the visitor's room is the cookie `wm_room`, set by /balcony/at/:handle and cleared by /balcony/at/house, so the client's fixed addresses reach the right room; every room opens and closes on the hours; `balcony_nights.scope`; the register's `balcony.night` carries scope. The door of a collection's room: stood before its work in the last three hours (`stood.last_at`, written on every standing; firsts.js), or made it, or the house; others see the window and a `.bc-door` line. /keys/at/:handle (keys.js): the club at one collection: the programme there, the viewing room, the benches (last lines), the tours, the balcony, the rights desk (institutions), the kids' corner, the team. The museum strip links it. Test: smoke 'the rooms, the clock and a museum's own house'.
- The clock (Will, 21 Oct: "a museum open 24/7, 365, with smart programming; somebody doesn't have to constantly be manning the chat room"; "day and late night get different content"; "fill the rooms with things as well as people; a kids' corner, all day"): `src/clock.js`. `programme(req, { maker })`: five slots by the visitor's own hour (`wm_tz` cookie, set in `layout` from the browser's zone; else New York): morning (a guided work read slowly, the Atelier, the kids' corner, a work for the easel), midday (a tour, today's work, the guides, the trade), evening (the wall of the night, a bench line, the balcony's hours, the next night), late (the balcony, the wall, a text, one more work), the small hours (the night room, one work to hold, a text); picks are deterministic by day and hour so a slot holds still; scoped to one maker when given. `clockBlock` (`.ck`) on /keys and /keys/at. /kids (`.kd`): six works for today marked for all and graded 4+ (or curated), the guide's first sentence, Find the three things / Draw it in five minutes (the easel), no prices, no bench; Wilhelm's line. Footer: Guides · The kids' corner.
- A museum's own house (Will, 21 Oct: "a Watermark Enterprise product… a white label, powered by Watermark"; "we earn their trust that way"): `src/tenants.js`. `makers.white_domain/white_on/white_accent`, set at /account/house (institutions, or any maker already on, or the Studio; CNAME steps on the page). A request on that domain (`req.tenant`; the map read once a minute; `houseHost` from BASE_URL is never a tenant) paints the layout (`V.setTenant`: the name as the wordmark with the portrait, the title's suffix, `--accent`/`--kred`, no house bar or house.js, footer '<Name> · Made with Watermark' and nothing else of ours) and rewrites the front: `/`, /collection, /makers, /artists → the maker's page; /stroll, /wander → ?maker=; /keys → /keys/at; /balcony → its own balcony unless a room is chosen. Every other address is the same house. /enterprise: the offer (what you get, what stays yours, what you are trusting; priced by letter; every word a draft). Footer: 'Your own house'. Test: smoke (the domain's front door, the rewrites, the house untouched).
- The host's chair (Will, 21 Oct: the rooms run by the clock, "but there will be somebody there to do it"; a career on the platform): `src/hosts.js`. `host_shifts` (scope house | m:<handle>, night (NY), owner m:<id> | b:<id>, status offered | taken | done | missed | paid, cents HOST_CENTS 5000 a draft). /hosts: the rota by first name, Take this night (a maker's or curator's key), Accept / Give it back; Studio → Back office → Hosts (/studio/hosts): offer the chair to a handle or email (letter + notice kind night), clear. The balcony honours the rota: POST /balcony/host admits tonight's host first (`rota`; the house always may); on close `hosts.settle` marks done and credits the pocket (`hosting`, cashable) from the house's side, or missed (the house mailed); `host.paid` hidden. A reminder the day of (`remind`, half-hourly). Test: smoke 'the host's chair'.
- The Screening Room (Will, 19–21 Oct: "not Netflix; films chosen by filmmakers and restorers, shown whole, 4K, plays through, a pause opens the filmmaker's notes; the Paris, the Metrograph at home"): `src/screening.js`, `public/screening.js`. `films` (slug, title, the filmmaker (and a maker if one), year, minutes, line, body, poster (a held work's slug or an address), `source_url` (https, an address the house controls, handed to <video> as it is: a 4K master is never proxied), notes 'mm:ss the note' a line, chosen by/why, status shelved | showing); `film_showings` (at, UTC from New York via `nyToUtc`; came), `film_seats`. /screening: the bill for the week and the films in the room; /screening/:slug: the lobby before the hour (poster, about, chosen by, next showing, three notes), the room during it for keyholders (the film from the showing's elapsed time, 'you join it in progress', no scrubbing (seeks undone), a pause shows the nearest note `.sc-note`, Resume), the window for others. Studio → Back office → Screening Room: shelve/edit, set a showing, clear. `screening.shown` public after each showing ('The Screening Room showed X by Y; N came'); `screening.shelved` hidden. The Keys' card opens (/screening, 'On the bill' when a showing is coming). Partnerships never claimed. Test: smoke 'the Screening Room'.
- The public domain and the reading room (Will, 21 Oct: "go get great books in the public domain, the books Franklin was interested in, and put them on our site"; "Gutenberg sits unused behind a shitty viewer; we build the most beautiful viewer for text"; "build out text, music and screening with the public domain, the best version of it"): `src/reading.js`, `public/read.js`. Studio → Back office → The public domain (/studio/reading): search Gutenberg (through Gutendex, GUTENDEX env) or a number → `bring(id)`: the plain text fetched from gutenberg.org, the START/END boilerplate and 'Produced by' cut (`strip`), chapters found (`chapters`: CHAPTER/BOOK/PART/LETTER lines, roman numerals, short capitals; tiny cuts merged), shelved as a `texts` row (kind book, body with '## ' heads, public, free (price_cents null), source the Gutenberg address, `text.shelved` with from:'gutenberg'); never twice. Franklin's shelf (`FRANKLIN`: the books he names in the Autobiography and his own, with why) as Find-it searches, never a guessed number. /read/:slug (`.rd`, html.rdp paper): Newsreader at 34em, running head, chapter number, drop cap, Chapters drawer, A− A+, Light · Sepia · Night (`wm_read_pref`), ← → j k, the place kept per book (`wm_read_<slug>`, 'Go back to your place'), the bar fades when still. The Library's text page offers 'Read it in the reading room' and folds a book's long body. Next: Standard Ebooks editions; the public domain into the Listening and Screening Rooms; the house voice reading a chapter. Test: smoke 'the public domain and the reading room'.
- The trade page went dark (21 Oct): the tour's page class `.tr` collided with the trade's `.tr` (For the trade); the tour is `.tw`/`tw-*` now (tours.js, tour.js, hangs.js, style.css, the dark-room regex in layout). Rule: a new room's class prefix is checked against style.css before it ships (`grep -c '^\.<prefix>' public/style.css`).
- The demo desk (21 Oct, for the Met's head of licensing): POST /studio/iiif/demo (a button on Studio → Collections in) makes the institution 'museum-demo' ('A museum desk (demonstration)', kind institution, its uses, rights desk open with a note that requests reach the house) and brings in up to `max` (100) of the Met's open-access highlights (isHighlight, hasImages, q=painting; CC0; shown at once). Named as a demonstration, never the Met's own door. `iiif_imports.max` caps a run. Test: smoke 'the Met, in'.
- An institution's page (21 Oct): `makerPage` reads as a museum for kind 'institution': kicker Institution, stats works · open access · collectors · hats (no split line), the Collect-and-license band becomes Open access / the rights desk, the house band says the collection is kept under the museum's name with /frame/tv?set=maker:<handle>, no patrons band. The museum strip gains 'On your television'.
- The introduction card (Will, 21 Oct, for the Leica Meatpacking evening, 22 Oct: a demo and photographers on board on the spot): GET /i/:token/card (the Studio for house links; a maker for their own): a printable page (`.ic`, paper) with the QR of the link (qr.js), 'Your studio, now.' and what happens on scanning; 'Card' beside each link on Studio → Accounts → Introductions. Make the link: Studio → Accounts → Introductions, label 'Leica Meatpacking', opens a maker, a cap; open its Card on the phone or print it. No deploys during the event (each deploy is a minute of 502).
- Classes, guarded (21 Oct, the third collision in a day: the card's `.ic` was the glyphs' class and stretched the header to a column of cream; the reading room's `.rd` was the rights desk's): the card is `.inc`, the reading room `.rdr` (html.rdp stays). `test/classes.js` (in npm test) fails when any bare class is defined more often than its baseline (`BASE`: the house's older classes that repeat for phones and dark rooms) or a new class twice; raise a baseline only for the same room's media query, never for another room.
- True size (Will, 21 Oct: "image corruption" on Wander, The Castle Against the Sun and a film portrait "absolutely terrible"; the served files are clean (no block structure, q90, the pictures gate) and the originals are 2,400-pixel web exports stretched across a 5K screen: the stretch is the damage): `public/truesize.js` (`WMTrue`, loaded in the head of every page): a picture is never shown larger than its pixels allow, a quarter's grace (UP 1.25) over one image pixel per device pixel, centred in the dark; `cap(img)` in the stroll (each screen, on make and on load), the viewing room (on load), `maxScale()` in the viewer's `center()`. A master with the pixels fills the screen as before. The real fix stays the full-size masters (Studio → Masters). sw.js `V` = wm-2026-10-21true.
- One word: Wander (Will, 21 Oct: "we have Stroll and Wander; we only need Wander; that's what we want people to do"): /wander is the stroll (stroll.js serves it; /stroll 301s to /wander with its query; the old /wander random-work redirect in app.js is gone); every address and every public word now says Wander ('Wander · Watermark', 'Wander your gallery', 'Wander the collection', 'Wander everything', 'Wander on', the Keys' Viewing Room card); /api/stroll, /stroll/side, the file names, classes (`.st-*`) and cookies (`wm_stroll_*`) stay as they are. The viewing room (/wander/room) remains for W and its own links but is no longer offered on the signs, the K ('Wander from here' → /wander?at=) or /join; tenants rewrite /wander to ?maker=; sw.js never caches /wander. Test: smoke (the door's /wander?in=mine).
